import app from "./app";
import { logger } from "./lib/logger";
import { pool, db, settingsTable, subscribersTable, trackedSignalsTable } from "@workspace/db";
import { setWebhook, registerBotCommands } from "./lib/telegram.js";
import { startScanner, stopScanner, getLastScanAt } from "./lib/scanner.js";
import { startWatcher, startWatchdog, broadcast } from "./lib/watcher.js";
import { startSpikeWatcher, stopSpikeWatcher } from "./lib/spike-watcher.js";
import { startMarketCapPreloader } from "./lib/whale_watch_v2.js";
import { lt, sql as drizzleSql, eq, inArray } from "drizzle-orm";

async function runStartupMigration() {
  try {
    // Добавляем колонку scanner_last_scan_at если ещё нет (идемпотентно)
    await db.execute(drizzleSql`ALTER TABLE settings ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS scanner_last_scan_at TIMESTAMP`);

    // Обновляем порог OI с 5% → 8% для сокращения отмен по слабым сигналам
    const updated = await db.update(settingsTable)
      .set({ oiChangePercent: 8 })
      .where(lt(settingsTable.oiChangePercent, 8))
      .returning({ oiChangePercent: settingsTable.oiChangePercent });
    if (updated.length > 0) logger.info({ newThreshold: 8 }, "Startup migration: oiChangePercent обновлён до 8%");
  } catch (err) {
    logger.warn({ err }, "Startup migration: ошибка (non-critical)");
  }

  // ── Одноразовая очистка: отменяем открытые сигналы старого whale_watch_spot ──
  // (паттерн переработан; старые сигналы с 39 накоплений мешают UI)
  try {
    const cancelled = await db.update(trackedSignalsTable)
      .set({
        status: "cancelled",
        cancelReason: "pattern_removed",
        closedAt: new Date(),
      })
      .where(
        eq(trackedSignalsTable.sentimentKey, "whale_watch_spot"),
      )
      .returning({ id: trackedSignalsTable.id });
    if (cancelled.length > 0) {
      logger.info({ count: cancelled.length }, "Startup cleanup: отменены старые whale_watch_spot сигналы");
    }
  } catch (err) {
    logger.warn({ err }, "Startup cleanup whale_watch_spot: ошибка (non-critical)");
  }

  // ── Seed known subscribers (идемпотентно через ON CONFLICT) ──────────────
  try {
    const knownSubs = [
      { chatId: 7818695412, username: "darshakl",    firstName: "Darya" },
      { chatId: 6964248629, username: "DaryaKach",   firstName: "Darya" },
    ];
    for (const sub of knownSubs) {
      await db.insert(subscribersTable)
        .values({ chatId: sub.chatId, username: sub.username, firstName: sub.firstName, lastName: null, active: true })
        .onConflictDoUpdate({
          target: subscribersTable.chatId,
          set: { active: true, username: sub.username, firstName: sub.firstName },
        });
    }
    logger.info({ count: knownSubs.length }, "Startup seed: known subscribers activated");
  } catch (err) {
    logger.warn({ err }, "Startup seed subscribers: ошибка (non-critical)");
  }
}


// ── pg Pool error handler ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Neon serverless периодически рвёт idle-соединения. Без обработчика 'error'
// Node.js бросает uncaughtException и процесс может упасть.
// Pool автоматически создаёт новое соединение при следующем запросе.
pool.on("error", (err) => {
  logger.warn({ err: String(err) }, "pg pool: idle client error (Neon closed connection) — pool will reconnect automatically");
});

// ── Глобальная защита от падения процесса ─────────────────────────────────────
// Без этого Node.js завершает процесс при любом необработанном промисе/исключении.
process.on("unhandledRejection", (reason, promise) => {
  logger.error({ reason, promise: String(promise) }, "⚠️ Unhandled promise rejection — caught, server stays alive");
});

process.on("uncaughtException", (err) => {
  logger.error({ err }, "⚠️ Uncaught exception — caught, server stays alive");
});

// ── Graceful shutdown при деплое (SIGTERM от Replit) ──────────────────────────
// Останавливаем планировщик сканера, даём текущим операциям завершиться (10 сек),
// затем выходим чисто — без обрыва на середине скана или отправки Telegram.
let isShuttingDown = false;
async function gracefulShutdown(signal: string) {
  if (isShuttingDown) return;
  isShuttingDown = true;
  logger.info({ signal }, "Graceful shutdown: получен сигнал, останавливаем сканер...");
  try {
    await stopScanner();
  } catch { /* non-critical */ }
  try {
    stopSpikeWatcher();
  } catch { /* non-critical */ }
  // Ждём 5 секунд чтобы текущие DB/Telegram операции завершились
  await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 5_000));
  logger.info("Graceful shutdown: завершено, выходим");
  process.exit(0);
}

process.on("SIGTERM", () => gracefulShutdown("SIGTERM"));
process.on("SIGINT",  () => gracefulShutdown("SIGINT"));

const rawPort = process.env["PORT"];

if (!rawPort) {
  throw new Error(
    "PORT environment variable is required but was not provided.",
  );
}

const port = Number(rawPort);

if (Number.isNaN(port) || port <= 0) {
  throw new Error(`Invalid PORT value: "${rawPort}"`);
}

app.listen(port, async (err) => {
  if (err) {
    logger.error({ err }, "Error listening on port");
    process.exit(1);
  }

  logger.info({ port }, "Server listening");

  // Webhook регистрируем ТОЛЬКО в production.
  // В dev-режиме dev-домен (.replit.dev) умирает при idle и перебивает
  // продакшн-webhook, из-за чего бот теряет все апдейты от Telegram.
  const domains = process.env.REPLIT_DOMAINS;
  if (domains && process.env.NODE_ENV !== "development") {
    const domain = domains.split(",")[0].trim();
    const webhookUrl = `https://${domain}/api/telegram/webhook`;
    const ok = await setWebhook(webhookUrl);
    if (ok) {
      logger.info({ webhookUrl }, "Telegram webhook registered");
    } else {
      logger.warn("Failed to register Telegram webhook — bot subscriptions may not work");
    }
    await registerBotCommands();
  } else if (process.env.NODE_ENV === "development") {
    logger.info("DEV mode — webhook registration skipped (production webhook preserved)");
  } else {
    logger.info("REPLIT_DOMAINS not set, skipping webhook registration");
  }

  // One-time migration: перенос позиций из dev в prod (30.05.2026)
  if (process.env.NODE_ENV !== "development") {
    runStartupMigration().catch(() => {/* logged inside */});
  }

  // В dev-режиме сканер и watcher НЕ запускаются — только production.
  // Причина: dev-инстанс подключён к тем же BingX API ключам и той же сети,
  // что и prod. Если оба экземпляра сканируют одновременно, они детектируют
  // сигналы в разное время и пишут в разные БД (dev vs prod).
  // Результат: дашборд (dev) и Telegram (prod) показывают разные позиции.
  // В dev нужно только разрабатывать код — реальный трейдинг идёт через prod.
  // MCap preloader — грузит CoinGlass/CoinGecko/CoinPaprika в память при старте,
  // обновляет каждые 6 часов. Скан loop никогда не делает HTTP для MCap самостоятельно.
  startMarketCapPreloader();

  if (process.env.NODE_ENV === "development") {
    logger.info("DEV mode — scanner NOT started; watcher started (Telegram suppressed)");
    // Watcher runs in dev to keep signal statuses accurate (stop_loss / profit / cancelled).
    // All Telegram sends are suppressed via sendMessage() guard in telegram.ts.
    startWatcher();
  } else {
    // Auto-start scanner on server boot
    startScanner().catch(err => logger.error({ err }, "Failed to auto-start scanner"));

    // Auto-start alpha watcher
    startWatcher();

    // Auto-start watchdog — alerts if stale, auto-restarts scanner after 2+ critical cycles
    const restartScanner = async () => {
      await stopScanner();
      await startScanner();
    };
    startWatchdog(getLastScanAt, restartScanner);

    // Auto-start real-time spike watcher (polls all tickers every 60s)
    startSpikeWatcher();
  }

  // Startup notification отправляется из sendBotRestartNotification() через 3 мин —
  // с контекстом открытых сигналов и DB-дедупликацией. Дублировать здесь не нужно.
});

FILE: artifacts/api-server/src/app.ts
================================================================
import express, { type Express } from "express";
import cors from "cors";
import pinoHttp from "pino-http";
import router from "./routes";
import { logger } from "./lib/logger";

const app: Express = express();

// Disable ETag — prevents 304 Not Modified caching of API responses in the dashboard
app.set("etag", false);

app.use(
  pinoHttp({
    logger,
    serializers: {
      req(req) {
        return {
          id: req.id,
          method: req.method,
          url: req.url?.split("?")[0],
        };
      },
      res(res) {
        return {
          statusCode: res.statusCode,
        };
      },
    },
  }),
);
app.use(cors());
app.use(express.json());
app.use(express.urlencoded({ extended: true }));

// Force no-cache on all API responses so the dashboard always gets fresh data
app.use("/api", (_, res, next) => {
  res.setHeader("Cache-Control", "no-store");
  next();
});

app.use("/api", router);

export default app;

import { Router } from "express";
import {
  getScannerState,
  startScanner,
  stopScanner,
  performScan,
  getScannerCandidates,
} from "../lib/scanner.js";
import { sendDailyReport, sendWeeklyDigest, runAuditReport } from "../lib/watcher.js";
import { getApiConnected } from "../lib/apiHealth.js";
import { getKlines } from "../lib/bingx.js";
import { db, prescanHistoryTable, oiWatchTable } from "@workspace/db";
import { eq, desc } from "drizzle-orm";

const router = Router();

router.get("/scanner/status", async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const state = await getScannerState();
    res.json({
      running: state.running,
      lastScanAt: state.lastScanAt?.toISOString() ?? null,
      nextScanAt: state.nextScanAt?.toISOString() ?? null,
      totalSignals: state.totalSignals,
      signalsToday: state.signalsToday,
      marketsScanned: state.marketsScanned,
      telegramConnected: state.telegramConnected,
      bingxConnected: state.bingxConnected,
      coinglassConnected:   getApiConnected("coinglass"),
      coinalyzeConnected:   getApiConnected("coinalyze"),
      coinGeckoConnected:   getApiConnected("coingecko"),
      coinPaprikaConnected: getApiConnected("coinpaprika"),
      scanIntervalMs: state.scanIntervalMs,
    });
  } catch (err) {
    req.log.error({ err }, "Failed to get scanner status");
    res.status(500).json({ error: "Failed to get scanner status" });
  }
});

router.post("/scanner/start", async (req, res) => {
  try {
    await startScanner();
    res.json({ success: true, message: "Scanner started" });
  } catch (err) {
    req.log.error({ err }, "Failed to start scanner");
    res.status(500).json({ success: false, message: "Failed to start scanner" });
  }
});

router.post("/scanner/stop", async (req, res) => {
  try {
    await stopScanner();
    res.json({ success: true, message: "Scanner stopped" });
  } catch (err) {
    req.log.error({ err }, "Failed to stop scanner");
    res.status(500).json({ success: false, message: "Failed to stop scanner" });
  }
});

router.post("/scanner/scan-now", async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const result = await performScan();
    res.json({
      success: true,
      signalsFound: result.signalsFound,
      marketsScanned: result.marketsScanned,
      durationMs: result.durationMs,
    });
  } catch (err) {
    req.log.error({ err }, "Failed to run scan");
    res.status(500).json({ success: false, signalsFound: 0, marketsScanned: 0, durationMs: 0 });
  }
});

router.get("/scanner/candidates", async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const data = await getScannerCandidates();
    res.json(data);
  } catch (err) {
    req.log.error({ err }, "Failed to get scanner candidates");
    res.status(500).json({ error: "Failed to get scanner candidates" });
  }
});

router.get("/scanner/symbol-history", async (req, res) => {
  const symbol = String(req.query.symbol ?? "");
  if (!symbol) {
    res.status(400).json({ error: "symbol is required" });
    return;
  }
  try {
    const [prescanHistory, oiWatchHistory] = await Promise.all([
      db.select().from(prescanHistoryTable)
        .where(eq(prescanHistoryTable.symbol, symbol))
        .orderBy(desc(prescanHistoryTable.ts))
        .limit(120),
      db.select({
        id:               oiWatchTable.id,
        triggerTf:        oiWatchTable.triggerTf,
        oiChange:         oiWatchTable.oiChange,
        priceAtEntry:     oiWatchTable.priceAtEntry,
        volumeMultiplier: oiWatchTable.volumeMultiplier,
        sentimentKey:     oiWatchTable.sentimentKey,
        status:           oiWatchTable.status,
        addedAt:          oiWatchTable.addedAt,
        expiresAt:        oiWatchTable.expiresAt,
        firedAt:          oiWatchTable.firedAt,
        oi5m:             oiWatchTable.oi5mChange,
        oi30m:            oiWatchTable.oi30mChange,
        oi1h:             oiWatchTable.oi1hChange,
        oi24h:            oiWatchTable.oi24hChange,
        price1hChange:    oiWatchTable.price1hChange,
        price24hChange:   oiWatchTable.price24hChange,
        volStatusReason:  oiWatchTable.volStatusReason,
      }).from(oiWatchTable)
        .where(eq(oiWatchTable.symbol, symbol))
        .orderBy(desc(oiWatchTable.addedAt))
        .limit(50),
    ]);

    res.json({
      symbol,
      prescanHistory: prescanHistory.map(r => ({
        ts:         r.ts.toISOString(),
        cycleCount: r.cycleCount,
        rank:       r.rank,
        score:      r.score,
        price24h:   r.price24h,
        price1h:    r.price1h,
        oi5m:       r.oi5m,
        oi30m:      r.oi30m,
        oi1h:       r.oi1h,
        oi4h:       r.oi4h,
        oi24h:      r.oi24h,
        volMult:    r.volMult,
        cvd15m:     r.cvd15m,
        cvd1h:      r.cvd1h,
      })),
      oiWatchHistory: oiWatchHistory.map(r => ({
        id:               r.id,
        triggerTf:        r.triggerTf,
        oiChange:         r.oiChange,
        priceAtEntry:     r.priceAtEntry,
        volumeMultiplier: r.volumeMultiplier,
        sentimentKey:     r.sentimentKey ?? null,
        status:           r.status,
        addedAt:          r.addedAt.toISOString(),
        expiresAt:        r.expiresAt.toISOString(),
        firedAt:          r.firedAt?.toISOString() ?? null,
        oi5m:             r.oi5m ?? null,
        oi30m:            r.oi30m ?? null,
        oi1h:             r.oi1h ?? null,
        oi24h:            r.oi24h ?? null,
        price1hChange:    r.price1hChange ?? null,
        price24hChange:   r.price24hChange ?? null,
        volStatusReason:  r.volStatusReason ?? null,
      })),
    });
  } catch (err) {
    req.log.error({ err }, "Failed to get symbol history");
    res.status(500).json({ error: "Failed to get symbol history" });
  }
});

    res.json({ success: true, message: "Audit started — отчёт придёт в канал через ~1-2 мин" });

      req.log.error({ err }, "Audit report failed");

// ── DEV: Ретроспективный анализ — исторические данные OI/CVD/Volume ──────────
// GET /api/scanner/debug/retro?symbol=SENT-USDT&fromSec=1749434400&toSec=1749456000
// Возвращает: Coinalyze OI history (30min) + BingX klines (30m) + CG OI snapshot + CG taker
router.get("/scanner/debug/retro", async (req, res) => {
  const symbol   = String(req.query.symbol   ?? "SENT-USDT");
  const fromSec  = Number(req.query.fromSec  ?? Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000) - 7200);
  const toSec    = Number(req.query.toSec    ?? Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000));
  const interval = String(req.query.interval ?? "30min");

  const CA_KEY = process.env.COINALYZE_API_KEY ?? "";
  const CG_KEY = process.env.COINGLASS_API_KEY ?? "";
  const base = symbol.replace("-USDT", "");

  // 1. Coinalyze OI history (30min candles, from/to in unix seconds)
  let oiHistory: { t: string; tSec: number; oi: number; oiChange: number }[] = [];
  try {
    const cgSym = `${base}USDT_PERP.A`;
    const url = new URL("https://api.coinalyze.net/v1/open-interest-history");
    url.searchParams.set("symbols",  cgSym);
    url.searchParams.set("interval", interval);
    url.searchParams.set("from",     String(fromSec));
    url.searchParams.set("to",       String(toSec));
    url.searchParams.set("api_key",  CA_KEY);
    const r = await fetch(url.toString(), { signal: AbortSignal.timeout(12000) });
    const j = await r.json() as { symbol: string; history: { t: number; o: number; h: number; l: number; c: number }[] }[];
    const rows = j?.[0]?.history ?? [];
    oiHistory = rows.map((c, i) => {
      const prev = i > 0 ? rows[i - 1].c : c.o;
      const changePct = prev > 0 ? ((c.c - prev) / prev * 100) : 0;
      return {
        t:        new Date(c.t * 1000).toUTCString().slice(5, 22),
        tSec:     c.t,
        oi:       Math.round(c.c),
        oiChange: parseFloat(changePct.toFixed(3)),
      };
    });
  } catch (err) {
    req.log.warn({ err }, "retro: Coinalyze failed");
  }

  // 2. CoinGlass OI snapshot (current multi-TF, public v2)
  let cgOI: { allRow: Record<string, unknown> | null; exchanges: { name: string; oiM: string; oi30m: number | null }[] } = { allRow: null, exchanges: [] };
  try {
    const r = await fetch(`https://open-api.coinglass.com/public/v2/open_interest?symbol=${base}`, {
      headers: { coinglassSecret: CG_KEY },
      signal: AbortSignal.timeout(8000),
    });
    const j = await r.json() as { data?: { exchangeName: string; openInterestAmount: number; m5OIChangePercent?: number; m30OIChangePercent?: number; h1OIChangePercent?: number; h4OIChangePercent?: number; oichangePercent?: number }[] };
    const rows = j?.data ?? [];
    const all  = rows.find(x => x.exchangeName === "All") ?? null;
    const exch = rows.filter(x => x.exchangeName !== "All" && x.openInterestAmount > 0);
    cgOI = {
      allRow:    all as Record<string, unknown> | null,
      exchanges: exch.map(x => ({ name: x.exchangeName, oiM: (x.openInterestAmount / 1e6).toFixed(2), oi30m: x.m30OIChangePercent ?? null })),
    };
  } catch (err) {
    req.log.warn({ err }, "retro: CoinGlass OI failed");
  }

  // 3. CoinGlass Taker flow — исторические свечи 15m по Binance+Bybit+OKX+Bitget
  // startTime/endTime в миллисекундах — получаем реальные исторические данные
  const cgTaker: { exch: string; candles: { t: string; tMs: number; buy: number; sell: number; net: number; netPct: number }[] }[] = [];
  const takerExchanges = ["Binance", "Bybit", "OKX", "Bitget"] as const;
  const takerSymMap: Record<string, string> = {
    Binance: `${base}USDT`,
    Bybit:   `${base}USDT`,
    OKX:     `${base}-USDT-SWAP`,
    Bitget:  `${base}USDT_UMCBL`,
  };
  // limit=64 → до 16 часов @ 15m
  const takerLimit = Math.min(64, Math.ceil((toSec - fromSec) / 900) + 4);
  await Promise.all(takerExchanges.map(async exch => {
    try {
      const url = new URL("https://open-api-v4.coinglass.com/api/futures/taker-buy-sell-volume/history");
      url.searchParams.set("symbol",    takerSymMap[exch]);
      url.searchParams.set("exchange",  exch);
      url.searchParams.set("interval",  "15m");
      url.searchParams.set("limit",     String(takerLimit));
      url.searchParams.set("startTime", String(fromSec * 1000));
      url.searchParams.set("endTime",   String(toSec   * 1000));
      const r = await fetch(url.toString(), {
        headers: { "CG-API-KEY": CG_KEY },
        signal: AbortSignal.timeout(8000),
      });
      const j = await r.json() as { code: string; data?: { time: number; taker_buy_volume_usd: string; taker_sell_volume_usd: string }[] };
      if (j.code === "0" && j.data?.length) {
        const candles = j.data
          .filter(c => c.time >= fromSec * 1000 && c.time <= toSec * 1000)
          .map(c => {
            const buy  = parseFloat(c.taker_buy_volume_usd);
            const sell = parseFloat(c.taker_sell_volume_usd);
            const net  = buy - sell;
            const tot  = buy + sell;
            return {
              t:      new Date(c.time).toUTCString().slice(5, 22),
              tMs:    c.time,
              buy:    Math.round(buy  / 1000),
              sell:   Math.round(sell / 1000),
              net:    Math.round(net  / 1000),
              netPct: tot > 0 ? parseFloat((net / tot * 100).toFixed(1)) : 0,
            };
          });
        if (candles.length) cgTaker.push({ exch, candles });
      }
    } catch { /* non-critical */ }
  }));

  // 4. BingX klines (30m)
  let bxKlines: { t: string; tMs: number; o: number; h: number; l: number; c: number; vol: number; priceChg: number }[] = [];
  try {
    const startMs = fromSec * 1000;
    const endMs   = toSec   * 1000;
    const url = `https://open-api.bingx.com/openApi/swap/v3/quote/klines?symbol=${symbol}&interval=30m&startTime=${startMs}&endTime=${endMs}&limit=100`;
    const r = await fetch(url, { signal: AbortSignal.timeout(10000) });
    const j = await r.json() as { data?: { time: number; open: string; high: string; low: string; close: string; volume: string }[] };
    bxKlines = (j?.data ?? [])
      .sort((a, b) => a.time - b.time)
      .map(k => ({
        t:        new Date(k.time).toUTCString().slice(5, 22),
        tMs:      k.time,
        o:        parseFloat(k.open),
        h:        parseFloat(k.high),
        l:        parseFloat(k.low),
        c:        parseFloat(k.close),
        vol:      parseFloat(k.volume),
        priceChg: parseFloat(((parseFloat(k.close) - parseFloat(k.open)) / parseFloat(k.open) * 100).toFixed(2)),
      }));
  } catch (err) {
    req.log.warn({ err }, "retro: BingX klines failed");
  }

  res.json({ symbol, fromSec, toSec, oiHistory, cgOI, cgTaker, bxKlines });
});

export default router;

FILE: artifacts/api-server/src/routes/settings.ts
================================================================
import { Router } from "express";
import { db, settingsTable } from "@workspace/db";
import { UpdateSettingsBody } from "@workspace/api-zod";

const router = Router();

async function getOrCreateSettings() {
  const rows = await db.select().from(settingsTable).limit(1);
  if (rows.length === 0) {
    const inserted = await db.insert(settingsTable).values({
      volumeMultiplier: 3,
      oiChangePercent: 10,
      fundingRateThreshold: 0.0005,
      scanIntervalMs: 300000,
      telegramEnabled: true,
      maxSymbols: 200,
    }).returning();
    return inserted[0];
  }
  return rows[0];
}

router.get("/settings", async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const settings = await getOrCreateSettings();
    res.json({
      volumeMultiplier: settings.volumeMultiplier,
      oiChangePercent: settings.oiChangePercent,
      fundingRateThreshold: settings.fundingRateThreshold,
      scanIntervalMs: settings.scanIntervalMs,
      telegramEnabled: settings.telegramEnabled,
      maxSymbols: settings.maxSymbols,
      channelId: settings.channelId ?? null,
      watchlist: settings.watchlist ?? null,
    });
  } catch (err) {
    req.log.error({ err }, "Failed to get settings");
    res.status(500).json({ error: "Failed to get settings" });
  }
});

router.put("/settings", async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const parsed = UpdateSettingsBody.safeParse(req.body);
    if (!parsed.success) {
      res.status(400).json({ error: "Invalid settings data" });
      return;
    }

    await getOrCreateSettings();
    const updates = parsed.data;

    // Auto-prepend @ to channel usernames that look like text handles
    if (updates.channelId && typeof updates.channelId === "string") {
      const ch = updates.channelId.trim();
      if (ch && !ch.startsWith("@") && !ch.startsWith("-") && isNaN(Number(ch))) {
        updates.channelId = `@${ch}`;
      }
    }

    const [updated] = await db.update(settingsTable)
      .set({
        ...updates,
        updatedAt: new Date(),
      })
      .returning();

    if (!updated) {
      res.status(404).json({ error: "Settings not found" });
      return;
    }

    res.json({
      volumeMultiplier: updated.volumeMultiplier,
      oiChangePercent: updated.oiChangePercent,
      fundingRateThreshold: updated.fundingRateThreshold,
      scanIntervalMs: updated.scanIntervalMs,
      telegramEnabled: updated.telegramEnabled,
      maxSymbols: updated.maxSymbols,
      channelId: updated.channelId ?? null,
      watchlist: updated.watchlist ?? null,
    });
  } catch (err) {
    req.log.error({ err }, "Failed to update settings");
    res.status(500).json({ error: "Failed to update settings" });
  }
});

export default router;

FILE: artifacts/api-server/src/routes/signals.ts

import { Router } from "express";
import { db, signalsTable, trackedSignalsTable, signalWhaleSeedsTable } from "@workspace/db";
import { desc, eq, gte, sql, and } from "drizzle-orm";
import { getTableColumns } from "drizzle-orm";

const router = Router();

router.get("/signals", async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const limit  = Math.min(parseInt(req.query.limit  as string ?? "50") || 50, 200);
    const offset = Math.max(parseInt(req.query.offset as string ?? "0")  || 0, 0);
    const type   = req.query.type   as string | undefined;
    const status = req.query.status as string | undefined;

    const conditions: ReturnType<typeof eq>[] = [];

    if (type && type !== "all") {
      conditions.push(
        eq(signalsTable.type, type as "volume_spike" | "oi_increase" | "funding_rate" | "strict_alpha" | "alpha" | "super_alpha" | "short_sniper")
      );
    }

    // Status filter via correlated subquery
    if (status && status !== "all") {
      if (status === "active") {
        conditions.push(sql`(
          SELECT COALESCE(status, 'active') FROM tracked_signals ts
          WHERE ts.signal_id = signals.id
          ORDER BY ts.id DESC LIMIT 1
        ) IN ('active', 'accumulation', 're_entry')` as any);
      } else {
        conditions.push(sql`(
          SELECT status FROM tracked_signals ts
          WHERE ts.signal_id = signals.id
          ORDER BY ts.id DESC LIMIT 1
        ) = ${status}` as any);
      }
    }

    const where = conditions.length > 0 ? and(...conditions) : undefined;

    const signals = await db
      .select({
        ...getTableColumns(signalsTable),
        // Tracker status fields
        trackedStatus: sql<string | null>`(
          SELECT status FROM tracked_signals ts
          WHERE ts.signal_id = signals.id
          ORDER BY ts.id DESC LIMIT 1
        )`,
        trackedPnl: sql<number | null>`(
          SELECT pnl_percent FROM tracked_signals ts
          WHERE ts.signal_id = signals.id
          ORDER BY ts.id DESC LIMIT 1
        )`,
        // Tracker position fields
        trackedEntryPrice: sql<number | null>`(
          SELECT entry_price FROM tracked_signals ts
          WHERE ts.signal_id = signals.id
          ORDER BY ts.id DESC LIMIT 1
        )`,
        trackedPeakPrice: sql<number | null>`(
          SELECT peak_price FROM tracked_signals ts
          WHERE ts.signal_id = signals.id
          ORDER BY ts.id DESC LIMIT 1
        )`,
        trackedTrailingStop: sql<number | null>`(
          SELECT trailing_stop_level FROM tracked_signals ts
          WHERE ts.signal_id = signals.id
          ORDER BY ts.id DESC LIMIT 1
        )`,
        trackedExitPrice: sql<number | null>`(
          SELECT exit_price FROM tracked_signals ts
          WHERE ts.signal_id = signals.id
          ORDER BY ts.id DESC LIMIT 1
        )`,
        trackedIsShort: sql<boolean>`COALESCE((
          SELECT is_short FROM tracked_signals ts
          WHERE ts.signal_id = signals.id
          ORDER BY ts.id DESC LIMIT 1
        ), false)`,
        // Whale seed flag
        isWhaleSeed: sql<boolean>`EXISTS (
          SELECT 1 FROM signal_whale_seeds
          WHERE signal_id = signals.id
        )`,
      })
      .from(signalsTable)
      .where(where)
      .orderBy(desc(signalsTable.detectedAt))
      .limit(limit)
      .offset(offset);

    const [totalResult] = await db.select({ count: sql<number>`count(*)` })
      .from(signalsTable)
      .where(where);

    res.json({
      signals: signals.map(s => ({
        ...s,
        detectedAt:          s.detectedAt.toISOString(),
        trackedStatus:       s.trackedStatus  ?? null,
        trackedPnl:          s.trackedPnl     ?? null,
        trackedEntryPrice:   s.trackedEntryPrice  ?? null,
        trackedPeakPrice:    s.trackedPeakPrice   ?? null,
        trackedTrailingStop: s.trackedTrailingStop ?? null,
        trackedExitPrice:    s.trackedExitPrice   ?? null,
        trackedIsShort:      Boolean(s.trackedIsShort),
        isWhaleSeed:         Boolean(s.isWhaleSeed),
      })),
      total: Number(totalResult.count),
    });
  } catch (err) {
    req.log.error({ err }, "Failed to get signals");
    res.status(500).json({ error: "Failed to get signals" });
  }
});

router.get("/signals/summary", async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const [totalResult] = await db.select({ count: sql<number>`count(*)` }).from(signalsTable);

    const today = new Date();
    today.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0);

    const [todayResult] = await db.select({ count: sql<number>`count(*)` })
      .from(signalsTable)
      .where(gte(signalsTable.detectedAt, today));

    const [strictAlphaResult] = await db.select({ count: sql<number>`count(*)` })
      .from(signalsTable)
      .where(and(eq(signalsTable.type, "strict_alpha"), gte(signalsTable.detectedAt, today)));

    const [strictAlphaTotalResult] = await db.select({ count: sql<number>`count(*)` })
      .from(signalsTable)
      .where(eq(signalsTable.type, "strict_alpha"));

    const topSymbolsRaw = await db.select({
      symbol: signalsTable.symbol,
      count: sql<number>`count(*)`,
    })
      .from(signalsTable)
      .where(and(eq(signalsTable.type, "strict_alpha"), gte(signalsTable.detectedAt, today)))
      .groupBy(signalsTable.symbol)
      .orderBy(desc(sql`count(*)`))
      .limit(5);

    res.json({
      totalSignals: Number(totalResult.count),
      signalsToday: Number(todayResult.count),
      strictAlphaToday: Number(strictAlphaResult.count),
      strictAlphaTotal: Number(strictAlphaTotalResult.count),
      volumeSpikes: Number(strictAlphaResult.count),
      oiIncreases: 0,
      fundingRateAlerts: 0,
      topSymbols: topSymbolsRaw.map(r => ({ symbol: r.symbol, count: Number(r.count) })),
    });
  } catch (err) {
    req.log.error({ err }, "Failed to get signals summary");
    res.status(500).json({ error: "Failed to get signals summary" });
  }
});

router.get("/signals/:id", async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const id = parseInt(req.params.id);
    if (isNaN(id)) {
      res.status(400).json({ error: "Invalid signal ID" });
      return;
    }

    const [signal] = await db.select().from(signalsTable).where(eq(signalsTable.id, id)).limit(1);

    if (!signal) {
      res.status(404).json({ error: "Signal not found" });
      return;
    }

    res.json({ ...signal, detectedAt: signal.detectedAt.toISOString() });
  } catch (err) {
    req.log.error({ err }, "Failed to get signal");
    res.status(500).json({ error: "Failed to get signal" });
  }
});

export default router;

FILE: artifacts/api-server/src/routes/subscribers.ts

import { Router } from "express";
import { db, subscribersTable, settingsTable, trackedSignalsTable, signalsTable, whaleFuturesWatchlistTable } from "@workspace/db";
import { and, desc, eq, gte, inArray, sql } from "drizzle-orm";
import { handleWebhookUpdate, sendMessage, answerCallbackQuery } from "../lib/telegram.js";
import { sendDailyReport, sendWeeklyDigest, sendMonthlyDigest } from "../lib/watcher.js";
import { buildPositionsStatusText } from "../lib/positionsReport.js";
import { getWhaleWatchlist, expireStaleWatchlist } from "../lib/whale_watch_v2.js";
import { getWhaleWatchState } from "../lib/scanner.js";
import { getFutureTicker, getFutureTickers } from "../lib/bingx.js";

const router = Router();

router.post("/telegram/webhook", async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const body = req.body as {
      callback_query?: {
        id: string;
        data?: string;
        message?: { chat?: { id?: number } };
        from?: { id?: number };
      };
    };

    if (body.callback_query) {
      const cq = body.callback_query;
      const chatId = cq.message?.chat?.id ?? cq.from?.id;
      const data = cq.data ?? "";

      await answerCallbackQuery(cq.id);

      if (chatId) {
        if (data === "payment_pending") {
          await sendMessage(chatId, [
            `⏳ <b>Оплата скоро будет доступна</b>`,
            ``,
            `Мы настраиваем систему оплаты — совсем скоро!`,
            `Следите за обновлениями.`,
          ].join("\n"));
        } else if (data === "menu:open_trades") {
          // Единый форматтер — тот же что в daily report, рестарт-уведомлении и 4ч апдейте
          const text = await buildPositionsStatusText();
          await sendMessage(chatId, text);
        } else if (data === "digest:daily") {
          await sendDailyReport(chatId);
        } else if (data === "digest:weekly") {
          await sendWeeklyDigest(chatId);
        } else if (data === "digest:monthly") {
          await sendMonthlyDigest(chatId);
        } else if (data === "whale_tracker") {
          await sendMessage(chatId, `🐋 Whale Watch 2.0 — анализирую...`);
          try {
            await expireStaleWatchlist();
            const watchlist = await getWhaleWatchlist();
            if (watchlist.length === 0) {
              await sendMessage(chatId, [
                `🐋 <b>Whale Watch 2.0</b>`,
                ``,
                `Активных накоплений не обнаружено.`,
                `Мониторинг работает в реальном времени — как только кит начнёт закупаться, токен появится здесь.`,
                ``,
                `Полный отчёт — каждую субботу в 10:00 МСК.`,
              ].join("\n"));
            } else {
              const top5 = watchlist.slice(0, 5);
              const lines: string[] = [`🐋 <b>Whale Watch 2.0 — ${watchlist.length} токенов</b>`, ``];
              for (const e of top5) {
                const coin = e.symbol.replace("-USDT", "");
                const cvdStr = e.accumulatedCvdUsd >= 1_000_000
                  ? `$${(e.accumulatedCvdUsd / 1_000_000).toFixed(1)}M`
                  : `$${(e.accumulatedCvdUsd / 1_000).toFixed(0)}K`;
                const accumPct = e.accumPct != null ? ` (${e.accumPct.toFixed(2)}% mcap)` : "";
                const emoji = e.status === "breakout" ? "🚀" : "🔵";
                lines.push(`${emoji} <b>${coin}</b> — CVD: ${cvdStr}${accumPct} | ${e.accumPeriods} периодов`);
              }
              if (watchlist.length > 5) lines.push(``, `...и ещё ${watchlist.length - 5} токенов`);
              lines.push(``, `🕒 Полный отчёт — Сб 10:00 МСК`);
              await sendMessage(chatId, lines.join("\n"));
            }
          } catch (err) {
            req.log.error({ err }, "whale_tracker callback error");
            await sendMessage(chatId, `❌ Ошибка при анализе. Попробуй позже.`);
          }
        } else if (data === "settings") {
          try {
            const rows = await db.select().from(settingsTable).limit(1);
            const s = rows[0];
            if (!s) {
              await sendMessage(chatId, `⚙️ <b>Настройки</b>\n\nНастройки не найдены в базе данных.`);
            } else {
              const intervalMin = Math.round(s.scanIntervalMs / 60000);
              const watchlistStr = s.watchlist ? s.watchlist.split(",").slice(0, 5).join(", ") + (s.watchlist.split(",").length > 5 ? " ..." : "") : "не задан";
              await sendMessage(chatId, [
                `⚙️ <b>Текущие настройки сканера</b>`,
                ``,
                `📊 <b>Фильтры сигналов:</b>`,
                `  Мин. объём: <b>${s.volumeMultiplier}x</b>`,
                `  Мин. OI: <b>+${s.oiChangePercent}%</b>`,
                `  Интервал скана: <b>${intervalMin} мин</b>`,
                `  Макс. символов: <b>${s.maxSymbols}</b>`,
                ``,
                `🚀 <b>Moonshot Momentum:</b>`,
                `  Объём ≥ ${s.moonshotVolMult ?? 5}x | Цена ≥ ${s.moonshotPriceChange ?? 15}% | OI ≥ ${s.moonshotOiChange ?? 10}%`,
                `  Блокировка стеной: $${((s.moonshotAskWallUsd ?? 50000) / 1000).toFixed(0)}k`,
                ``,
                `🛡 <b>Smart Cancel:</b>`,
                `  Мин. бид-стена: $${((s.smartCancelBidWallUsd ?? 50000) / 1000).toFixed(0)}k`,
                `  Мин. OI 4h для блокировки: ${s.smartCancelOiPct ?? 5}%`,
                ``,
                `📢 Telegram: <b>${s.telegramEnabled ? "включён ✅" : "выключен ❌"}</b>`,
                s.channelId ? `📡 Канал: <code>${s.channelId}</code>` : `📡 Канал: <i>не настроен</i>`,
                s.watchlist ? `📋 Watchlist: <code>${watchlistStr}</code>` : ``,
                ``,
                `Изменить настройки: <b>bingx-trader.replit.app</b> → Settings`,
              ].filter(Boolean).join("\n"));
            }
          } catch (err) {
            req.log.error({ err }, "settings callback error");
            await sendMessage(chatId, `❌ Ошибка при загрузке настроек. Попробуй позже.`);
          }
        }
      }

      return res.json({ ok: true });
    }

    // ── /whalewatch — текущий watchlist Whale Watch 2.0 ──────────────────────
    const msgText = (req.body as { message?: { text?: string; chat?: { id?: number } } })?.message;
    if (msgText?.text?.toLowerCase().startsWith("/whalewatch")) {
      const chatId = msgText.chat?.id;
      if (chatId) {
        await sendMessage(chatId, `🐋 Whale Watch 2.0 — загружаю watchlist...`);
        try {
          await expireStaleWatchlist();
          const watchlist = await getWhaleWatchlist();
          if (watchlist.length === 0) {
            await sendMessage(chatId, [
              `🐋 <b>Whale Watch 2.0</b>`,
              ``,
              `Активных накоплений не обнаружено.`,
              `Мониторинг работает в реальном времени.`,
            ].join("\n"));
          } else {
            const lines = [`🐋 <b>Whale Watch 2.0 — ${watchlist.length} токенов</b>`, ``];
            for (const e of watchlist.slice(0, 10)) {
              const coin = e.symbol.replace("-USDT", "");
              const cvdStr = e.accumulatedCvdUsd >= 1_000_000
                ? `$${(e.accumulatedCvdUsd / 1_000_000).toFixed(1)}M`
                : `$${(e.accumulatedCvdUsd / 1_000).toFixed(0)}K`;
              const pct = e.accumPct != null ? ` (${e.accumPct.toFixed(2)}% mcap)` : "";
              const emoji = e.status === "breakout" ? "🚀" : "🔵";
              lines.push(`${emoji} <b>${coin}</b> — ${cvdStr}${pct} | ${e.accumPeriods}п`);
            }
            if (watchlist.length > 10) lines.push(``, `...ещё ${watchlist.length - 10}`);
            await sendMessage(chatId, lines.join("\n"));
          }
        } catch (err) {
          req.log.error({ err }, "/whalewatch error");
          await sendMessage(chatId, `❌ Ошибка. Попробуй позже.`);
        }
        return res.json({ ok: true });
      }
    }

    // ── /addsignal — ручной импорт позиции прямо из Telegram ────────────────
    // Формат: /addsignal SYMBOL PRICE STATUS
    // STATUS: active | in_run | re_entry
    // Только для владельца (проверка по TELEGRAM_CHAT_ID)
    if (msgText?.text?.toLowerCase().startsWith("/addsignal")) {
      const chatId = msgText.chat?.id;
      if (!chatId) return res.json({ ok: true });

      const ownerRaw = process.env.TELEGRAM_CHAT_ID?.trim();
      const isOwner = ownerRaw && !ownerRaw.startsWith("@") && String(chatId) === ownerRaw;
      if (!isOwner) {
        await sendMessage(chatId, `❌ Команда доступна только владельцу бота.`);
        return res.json({ ok: true });
      }

      try {
        const parts = msgText.text.trim().split(/\s+/);
        if (parts.length < 4) {
          await sendMessage(chatId, [
            `📥 <b>/addsignal — ручной импорт сигнала</b>`,
            ``,
            `<b>Формат:</b> <code>/addsignal SYMBOL PRICE STATUS</code>`,
            ``,
            `<b>Примеры:</b>`,
            `<code>/addsignal EPIC-USDT 0.45 in_run</code>`,
            `<code>/addsignal SUSHI-USDT 0.84 active</code>`,
            `<code>/addsignal ZBT-USDT 0.127 active</code>`,
            ``,
            `<b>STATUS:</b> active | in_run | re_entry`,
          ].join("\n"));
          return res.json({ ok: true });
        }

        let symbol = parts[1].toUpperCase();
        if (!symbol.endsWith("-USDT")) symbol = `${symbol}-USDT`;
        const price = parseFloat(parts[2]);
        const status = parts[3].toLowerCase() as "active" | "in_run" | "re_entry";

        if (isNaN(price) || price <= 0) {
          await sendMessage(chatId, `❌ Неверная цена: <code>${parts[2]}</code>\nОжидается число > 0.`);
          return res.json({ ok: true });
        }

        const validStatuses = ["active", "in_run", "re_entry"];
        if (!validStatuses.includes(status)) {
          await sendMessage(chatId, `❌ Неверный статус: <code>${parts[3]}</code>\nДоступные: active | in_run | re_entry`);
          return res.json({ ok: true });
        }

        // Проверяем нет ли уже активного сигнала по этому символу
        const allOpen = await db.select().from(trackedSignalsTable)
          .where(inArray(trackedSignalsTable.status, ["active", "in_run", "accumulation", "re_entry"]));
        const existing = allOpen.filter(r => r.symbol === symbol);
        if (existing.length > 0) {
          const e = existing[0];
          await sendMessage(chatId, [
            `⚠️ <b>${symbol}</b> уже отслеживается`,
            ``,
            `ID: ${e.id} | Статус: <b>${e.status}</b> | Вход: $${e.entryPrice}`,
            `Создан: ${e.detectedAt.toLocaleDateString("ru-RU")}`,
            ``,
            `Если нужно добавить заново — сначала дождись закрытия или напиши мне.`,
          ].join("\n"));
          return res.json({ ok: true });
        }

        // Создаём строку в signals (как «anchor» для внешнего ключа)
        const tvSymbol = symbol.replace("-", "");
        const [sig] = await db.insert(signalsTable).values({
          symbol,
          type: "alpha",
          value: 0,
          threshold: 0,
          price,
          priceChangePercent: 0,
          volume24h: 0,
          avgVolume: 0,
          tradingViewUrl: `https://www.tradingview.com/chart/?symbol=BINGX:${tvSymbol}`,
          sentiment: "manual",
          sentimentKey: "manual_import",
        }).returning();

        // Создаём строку в tracked_signals
        const [tracked] = await db.insert(trackedSignalsTable).values({
          symbol,
          signalId: sig.id,
          signalType: "alpha",
          sentimentKey: "manual_import",
          entryPrice: price,
          peakPrice: price,
          status,
        }).returning();

        req.log.info({ symbol, price, status, signalId: sig.id, trackedId: tracked.id }, "/addsignal: manual import via Telegram");

        const statusEmoji: Record<string, string> = { active: "🟡", in_run: "🚀", accumulation: "🔵", re_entry: "🔄" };
        await sendMessage(chatId, [
          `✅ <b>Сигнал добавлен в базу</b>`,
          ``,
          `${statusEmoji[status] ?? "⚪"} <b>${symbol}</b>`,
          `💰 Вход: <b>$${price}</b>`,
          `📊 Статус: <b>${status}</b>`,
          ``,
          `signal_id: <code>${sig.id}</code> | tracked_id: <code>${tracked.id}</code>`,
          ``,
          `Вотчер подхватит позицию в следующем цикле (~2 мин).`,
        ].join("\n"));
      } catch (err) {
        req.log.error({ err }, "/addsignal error");
        await sendMessage(chatId, `❌ Ошибка при добавлении сигнала. Детали в логах сервера.`);
      }
      return res.json({ ok: true });
    }

    await handleWebhookUpdate(req.body);
    return res.json({ ok: true });
  } catch (err) {
    req.log.error({ err }, "Webhook processing error");
    return res.json({ ok: true });
  }
});

router.get("/subscribers", async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const subscribers = await db.select().from(subscribersTable)
      .orderBy(subscribersTable.subscribedAt);

    const activeCount = subscribers.filter(s => s.active).length;

    res.json({
      subscribers: subscribers.map(s => ({
        chatId: s.chatId,
        username: s.username,
        firstName: s.firstName,
        lastName: s.lastName,
        active: s.active,
        subscribedAt: s.subscribedAt.toISOString(),
        unsubscribedAt: s.unsubscribedAt?.toISOString() ?? null,
      })),
      total: subscribers.length,
      active: activeCount,
    });
  } catch (err) {
    req.log.error({ err }, "Failed to get subscribers");
    res.status(500).json({ error: "Failed to get subscribers" });
  }
});

router.delete("/subscribers/:chatId", async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const chatId = Number(req.params.chatId);
    if (isNaN(chatId)) {
      res.status(400).json({ error: "Invalid chatId" });
      return;
    }
    await db.update(subscribersTable)
      .set({ active: false, unsubscribedAt: new Date() })
      .where(eq(subscribersTable.chatId, chatId));
    req.log.info({ chatId }, "Subscriber removed via admin API");
    res.json({ ok: true, chatId });
  } catch (err) {
    req.log.error({ err }, "Failed to remove subscriber");
    res.status(500).json({ error: "Failed to remove subscriber" });
  }
});

router.get("/subscribers/stats", async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const [totalResult] = await db.select({ count: sql<number>`count(*)` }).from(subscribersTable);
    const [activeResult] = await db.select({ count: sql<number>`count(*)` })
      .from(subscribersTable).where(eq(subscribersTable.active, true));

    const today = new Date();
    today.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0);
    const [todayResult] = await db.select({ count: sql<number>`count(*)` })
      .from(subscribersTable).where(gte(subscribersTable.subscribedAt, today));

    const weekAgo = new Date(Date.now() - 7 * 24 * 3600_000);
    const [weekResult] = await db.select({ count: sql<number>`count(*)` })
      .from(subscribersTable).where(gte(subscribersTable.subscribedAt, weekAgo));

    const total = Number(totalResult.count);
    const active = Number(activeResult.count);

    res.json({
      total,
      active,
      inactive: total - active,
      newToday: Number(todayResult.count),
      newThisWeek: Number(weekResult.count),
    });
  } catch (err) {
    req.log.error({ err }, "Failed to get subscriber stats");
    res.status(500).json({ error: "Failed to get subscriber stats" });
  }
});

// ── Whale Watch 2.0 API ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
router.get("/whale-watch", async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const { stages, pathA } = getWhaleWatchState();
    await expireStaleWatchlist();
    const [watchlist, spotWatching, spotFiredRaw, activeSpotRaw] = await Promise.all([
      getWhaleWatchlist(),
      db.select().from(whaleFuturesWatchlistTable)
        .where(and(
          eq(whaleFuturesWatchlistTable.type, "spot"),
          eq(whaleFuturesWatchlistTable.status, "watching"),
        ))
        .orderBy(desc(whaleFuturesWatchlistTable.detectedAt)),
      db.select().from(whaleFuturesWatchlistTable)
        .where(and(
          eq(whaleFuturesWatchlistTable.type, "spot"),
          eq(whaleFuturesWatchlistTable.status, "fired"),
        ))
        .orderBy(desc(whaleFuturesWatchlistTable.firedAt))
        .limit(10),
      db.select().from(trackedSignalsTable)
        .where(and(
          eq(trackedSignalsTable.sentimentKey, "whale_watch_spot"),
          inArray(trackedSignalsTable.status, ["active", "in_run"]),
        ))
        .orderBy(desc(trackedSignalsTable.detectedAt)),
    ]);

    // Compute live PnL for each fired entry (current price vs priceAtDetection)
    const spotFired = await Promise.all(spotFiredRaw.map(async (entry) => {
      try {
        const ticker = await getFutureTicker(entry.symbol);
        const currentPrice = ticker ? parseFloat(ticker.lastPrice) : null;
        const currentPnl = (currentPrice != null && entry.priceAtDetection > 0)
          ? ((currentPrice - entry.priceAtDetection) / entry.priceAtDetection) * 100
          : null;
        return { ...entry, currentPnl };
      } catch {
        return { ...entry, currentPnl: null };
      }
    }));

    // Enrich active spot tracked signals with live prices from BingX
    let priceMap = new Map<string, number>();
    if (activeSpotRaw.length > 0) {
      try {
        const tickers = await getFutureTickers() as { symbol: string; lastPrice: string }[];
        for (const t of tickers) priceMap.set(t.symbol, parseFloat(t.lastPrice) || 0);
      } catch { /* non-critical — show without live price */ }
    }

    const now = Date.now();
    const activeSpotSignals = activeSpotRaw.map((s) => {
      const currentPrice = priceMap.get(s.symbol) ?? null;
      const pnlPercent = (currentPrice != null && s.entryPrice > 0)
        ? ((currentPrice - s.entryPrice) / s.entryPrice) * 100
        : null;
      const peakPnl = (s.peakPrice != null && s.entryPrice > 0)
        ? ((s.peakPrice - s.entryPrice) / s.entryPrice) * 100
        : null;
      const daysHeld = (now - s.detectedAt.getTime()) / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24);
      return {
        id: s.id,
        symbol: s.symbol,
        detectedAt: s.detectedAt.toISOString(),
        entryPrice: s.entryPrice,
        currentPrice,
        pnlPercent,
        daysHeld,
        peakPnl,
        status: s.status,
      };
    });

    res.json({ stages, pathA, watchlist, spotWatching, spotFired, activeSpotSignals });
  } catch (err) {
    req.log.error({ err }, "Failed to get whale watch data");
    res.status(500).json({ error: "Failed to get whale watch data" });
  }
});

// ── Channel test ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
router.post("/telegram/test-channel", async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const rows = await db.select({ channelId: settingsTable.channelId }).from(settingsTable).limit(1);
    const channelId = rows[0]?.channelId ?? null;

    if (!channelId) {
      res.json({ success: false, message: "Channel ID не настроен в Settings" });
      return;
    }

    const testText = [
      `✅ <b>Тест канала BingX AltBullet</b>`,
      ``,
      `Канал: <code>${channelId}</code>`,
      `Время: ${new Date().toUTCString()}`,
      ``,
      `Если видишь это — бот корректно пересылает сигналы в этот канал.`,
    ].join("\n");

    const ok = await sendMessage(channelId, testText);

    if (ok) {
      res.json({ success: true, message: `Тестовое сообщение отправлено в ${channelId}` });
    } else {
      res.json({
        success: false,
        message: "Не удалось отправить. Проверь: бот добавлен в канал как Admin с правом 'Post Messages'",
      });
    }
  } catch (err) {
    req.log.error({ err }, "Channel test error");
    res.status(500).json({ success: false, message: "Ошибка сервера" });
  }
});

export default router;


================================================================
FILE: artifacts/api-server/src/routes/tracker.ts

import { Router } from "express";
import { db, trackedSignalsTable, signalWhaleSeedsTable } from "@workspace/db";
import type { TrackedSignal } from "@workspace/db";
import { desc, eq, gte, ne, and, inArray, sql } from "drizzle-orm";
import { runWatcherCycle, sendWeeklyDigest, sendDailyReport, adminBroadcast } from "../lib/watcher.js";
import { getLiveMarketData } from "../lib/scanner.js";
import { buildPositionsStatusText } from "../lib/positionsReport.js";
import { getFutureTickers } from "../lib/bingx.js";
import { getCoinglassSpotVolume24h } from "../lib/coinglass_spot_cvd.js";
import { batchFetchMarketCap } from "../lib/whale_watch_v2.js";
import { logger } from "../lib/logger.js";
import { TRAILING_ACTIVATION_PCT, TRAILING_FIXED_PCT } from "../lib/watcher-config.js";

const router = Router();

const OPEN_STATUSES = ["active", "in_run", "accumulation", "re_entry"] as const;

// GET /api/tracker — list tracked signals
router.get("/tracker", async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const status = req.query.status as string | undefined;
    const limit = Math.min(parseInt(req.query.limit as string ?? "100") || 100, 200);

    const where = status && status !== "all"
      ? eq(trackedSignalsTable.status, status as any)
      : undefined;

    const signals = await db.select()
      .from(trackedSignalsTable)
      .where(where)
      .orderBy(desc(trackedSignalsTable.detectedAt))
      .limit(limit);

    // For open positions fetch live prices from BingX to compute real-time PnL
    const priceMap = new Map<string, number>();
    const openSignals = signals.filter(s => OPEN_STATUSES.includes(s.status as any));
    if (openSignals.length > 0) {
      try {
        const tickers = await getFutureTickers() as { symbol: string; lastPrice: string }[];
        for (const t of tickers) priceMap.set(t.symbol, parseFloat(t.lastPrice) || 0);
      } catch { /* non-critical — show without live price */ }
    }

    // Enrich open positions with live OI + CVD data from scanner prescan tracker
    const liveMarket = getLiveMarketData(openSignals.map(s => s.symbol));

    res.json({
      signals: signals.map(s => {
        const base = {
          ...s,
          detectedAt: s.detectedAt.toISOString(),
          updatedAt:  s.updatedAt.toISOString(),
          closedAt:   s.closedAt?.toISOString() ?? null,
        };
        if (OPEN_STATUSES.includes(s.status as any) && s.entryPrice != null) {
          const currentPrice = priceMap.get(s.symbol) ?? null;
          const entry = Number(s.entryPrice);
          const livePnl = currentPrice && entry > 0
            ? Math.round(((currentPrice - entry) / entry) * 10000) / 100
            : null;
          // Live peak = max(current price, stored peak) so dashboard always reflects reality
          const dbPeak = s.peakPrice != null ? Number(s.peakPrice) : null;
          const livePeak = currentPrice
            ? (dbPeak && dbPeak > currentPrice ? dbPeak : currentPrice)
            : dbPeak;
          const peakPnl = livePeak && entry > 0
            ? ((livePeak - entry) / entry) * 100
            : 0;
          // Trailing stop: activated once peak PnL >= TRAILING_ACTIVATION_PCT
          const liveTrailingStop = peakPnl >= TRAILING_ACTIVATION_PCT && livePeak
            ? Math.round(livePeak * (1 - TRAILING_FIXED_PCT / 100) * 1e8) / 1e8
            : null;
          return {
            ...base,
            pnlPercent: livePnl,
            currentPrice,
            peakPrice: livePeak,
            trailingStopLevel: liveTrailingStop,
            ...(liveMarket[s.symbol] ?? {}),
          };
        }
        return base;
      }),
      total: signals.length,
    });
  } catch (err) {
    res.status(500).json({ error: "Failed to fetch tracked signals" });
  }
});

// GET /api/tracker/journal — full trading journal data
// Optional query param: ?from=YYYY-MM-DD
//   - Open signals (active/accumulation/in_run/re_entry): always counted without date filter
//   - Closed signals (profit/stop_loss/cancelled/breakeven): filtered by closed_at >= from
router.get("/tracker/journal", async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const fromParam = req.query.from as string | undefined;
    const fromDate  = fromParam ? new Date(fromParam) : null;
    // closedFilter applies only to closed signals — filter by when they CLOSED, not when detected
    const closedFilter = fromDate ? gte(trackedSignalsTable.closedAt, fromDate) : undefined;

    // 1a. Open signals — always count ALL open regardless of period filter
    // Excluding whale_watch_spot (long-term spot accumulation tracked separately on Whale Watch page)
    const openStatusRows = await db.select({
      status: trackedSignalsTable.status,
      count: sql<number>`count(*)::int`,
    })
    .from(trackedSignalsTable)
    .where(and(
      inArray(trackedSignalsTable.status, ["active", "accumulation", "in_run", "re_entry"]),
      ne(trackedSignalsTable.sentimentKey, "whale_watch_spot"),
    ))
    .groupBy(trackedSignalsTable.status);

    const byOpen: Record<string, number> = {};
    for (const row of openStatusRows) {
      byOpen[row.status ?? "unknown"] = row.count;
    }
    const active = (byOpen["active"] ?? 0) + (byOpen["accumulation"] ?? 0) + (byOpen["in_run"] ?? 0) + (byOpen["re_entry"] ?? 0);

    // 1b. Closed signals — filter by closedAt >= from
    const closedStatusRows = await db.select({
      status: trackedSignalsTable.status,
      count: sql<number>`count(*)::int`,
      avgPnl: sql<number>`round(avg(pnl_percent)::numeric, 2)`,
    })
    .from(trackedSignalsTable)
    .where(and(
      inArray(trackedSignalsTable.status, ["profit", "stop_loss", "cancelled", "breakeven", "bu_stop"]),
      ne(trackedSignalsTable.sentimentKey, "whale_watch_spot"),
      closedFilter,
    ))
    .groupBy(trackedSignalsTable.status);

    const byStatus: Record<string, { count: number; avgPnl: number | null }> = {};
    for (const row of closedStatusRows) {
      byStatus[row.status ?? "unknown"] = { count: row.count, avgPnl: row.avgPnl != null ? Number(row.avgPnl) : null };
    }

    const profit    = byStatus["profit"]?.count    ?? 0;
    const stopLoss  = byStatus["stop_loss"]?.count  ?? 0;
    const cancelled = byStatus["cancelled"]?.count  ?? 0;
    const breakeven = byStatus["breakeven"]?.count  ?? 0;
    const buStop    = byStatus["bu_stop"]?.count    ?? 0;
    const total     = active + profit + stopLoss + cancelled + breakeven + buStop;
    const closed    = profit + stopLoss + breakeven + buStop;
    // Win rate = (profit + buStop) / (profit + buStop + stopLoss) — buStop считается как выигрыш (защищённая позиция)
    const winRateDenominator = profit + buStop + stopLoss;
    const winRate   = winRateDenominator > 0 ? Math.round(((profit + buStop) / winRateDenominator) * 100) : null;
    const avgProfit = byStatus["profit"]?.avgPnl ?? null;
    const avgLoss   = byStatus["stop_loss"]?.avgPnl ?? null;

    // 2. Best / worst trade — filter by closedAt >= from
    const bestRows = await db.select({
      symbol: trackedSignalsTable.symbol,
      pnl: trackedSignalsTable.pnlPercent,
      sentimentKey: trackedSignalsTable.sentimentKey,
    })
    .from(trackedSignalsTable)
    .where(and(eq(trackedSignalsTable.status, "profit"), ne(trackedSignalsTable.sentimentKey, "whale_watch_spot"), closedFilter))
    .orderBy(desc(trackedSignalsTable.pnlPercent))
    .limit(1);

    const worstRows = await db.select({
      symbol: trackedSignalsTable.symbol,
      pnl: trackedSignalsTable.pnlPercent,
      sentimentKey: trackedSignalsTable.sentimentKey,
    })
    .from(trackedSignalsTable)
    .where(and(eq(trackedSignalsTable.status, "stop_loss"), ne(trackedSignalsTable.sentimentKey, "whale_watch_spot"), closedFilter))
    .orderBy(trackedSignalsTable.pnlPercent)
    .limit(1);

    // 3. Per-pattern breakdown — closed signals only, filtered by closedAt >= from
    const patternRows = await db.select({
      sentimentKey: trackedSignalsTable.sentimentKey,
      status: trackedSignalsTable.status,
      count: sql<number>`count(*)::int`,
      avgPnl:  sql<number>`round(avg(pnl_percent)::numeric, 2)`,
      avgPeak: sql<number>`round(avg((peak_price - entry_price) / nullif(entry_price, 0) * 100)::numeric, 2)`,
      lastSignalAt: sql<string>`max(detected_at)`,
    })
    .from(trackedSignalsTable)
    .where(and(
      inArray(trackedSignalsTable.status, ["profit", "stop_loss", "cancelled", "breakeven", "bu_stop"]),
      ne(trackedSignalsTable.sentimentKey, "whale_watch_spot"),
      closedFilter,
    ))
    .groupBy(trackedSignalsTable.sentimentKey, trackedSignalsTable.status);

    const patternMap = new Map<string, { profit: number; stopLoss: number; cancelled: number; breakeven: number; buStop: number; totalPnl: number; pnlCount: number; totalPeak: number; peakCount: number; lastSignalAt: string | null }>();
    for (const row of patternRows) {
      const key = row.sentimentKey ?? "unknown";
      if (!patternMap.has(key)) patternMap.set(key, { profit: 0, stopLoss: 0, cancelled: 0, breakeven: 0, buStop: 0, totalPnl: 0, pnlCount: 0, totalPeak: 0, peakCount: 0, lastSignalAt: null });
      const p = patternMap.get(key)!;
      if (row.status === "profit")         p.profit    += row.count;
      else if (row.status === "stop_loss") p.stopLoss  += row.count;
      else if (row.status === "cancelled") p.cancelled += row.count;
      else if (row.status === "breakeven") p.breakeven += row.count;
      else if (row.status === "bu_stop")   p.buStop    += row.count;
      if (row.avgPnl  != null) { p.totalPnl  += Number(row.avgPnl)  * row.count; p.pnlCount  += row.count; }
      if (row.avgPeak != null) { p.totalPeak += Number(row.avgPeak) * row.count; p.peakCount += row.count; }
      if (row.lastSignalAt != null && (p.lastSignalAt == null || row.lastSignalAt > p.lastSignalAt)) {
        p.lastSignalAt = row.lastSignalAt as string;
      }
    }

    const byPattern = Array.from(patternMap.entries()).map(([key, p]) => {
      const patTotal   = p.profit + p.stopLoss + p.cancelled + p.breakeven + p.buStop;
      const patWrDenom = p.profit + p.buStop + p.stopLoss;
      return {
        sentimentKey: key,
        total:    patTotal,
        profit:   p.profit,
        stopLoss: p.stopLoss,
        cancelled: p.cancelled,
        buStop:   p.buStop,
        winRate:  patWrDenom > 0 ? Math.round(((p.profit + p.buStop) / patWrDenom) * 100) : null,
        avgPnl:   p.pnlCount  > 0 ? Math.round((p.totalPnl  / p.pnlCount)  * 10) / 10 : null,
        avgPeak:  p.peakCount > 0 ? Math.round((p.totalPeak / p.peakCount) * 10) / 10 : null,
        lastSignalAt: p.lastSignalAt,
      };
    }).sort((a, b) => b.total - a.total);

    // 4. Per-token breakdown — closed signals only, filtered by closedAt >= from
    const tokenRows = await db.execute(
      fromDate
        ? sql`
          SELECT
            symbol,
            COUNT(*)::int                                                                         AS total,
            COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE status = 'profit')::int                                       AS profit,
            COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE status = 'stop_loss')::int                                    AS stop_loss,
            COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE status = 'cancelled')::int                                    AS cancelled,
            COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE status = 'breakeven')::int                                    AS breakeven,
            ROUND(AVG(pnl_percent)::numeric, 2)                                                  AS avg_pnl,
            ROUND(MAX(pnl_percent) FILTER (WHERE status = 'profit')::numeric, 2)                AS best_pnl,
            ROUND(AVG((peak_price - entry_price) / NULLIF(entry_price, 0) * 100)::numeric, 2)   AS avg_peak,
            MODE() WITHIN GROUP (ORDER BY sentiment_key)                                         AS main_pattern,
            MAX(detected_at)                                                                      AS last_signal_at
          FROM tracked_signals
          WHERE status IN ('profit', 'stop_loss', 'cancelled', 'breakeven')
            AND closed_at >= ${fromDate}
          GROUP BY symbol
          ORDER BY total DESC
        `
        : sql`
          SELECT
            symbol,
            COUNT(*)::int                                                                         AS total,
            COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE status = 'profit')::int                                       AS profit,
            COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE status = 'stop_loss')::int                                    AS stop_loss,
            COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE status = 'cancelled')::int                                    AS cancelled,
            COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE status = 'breakeven')::int                                    AS breakeven,
            ROUND(AVG(pnl_percent)::numeric, 2)                                                  AS avg_pnl,
            ROUND(MAX(pnl_percent) FILTER (WHERE status = 'profit')::numeric, 2)                AS best_pnl,
            ROUND(AVG((peak_price - entry_price) / NULLIF(entry_price, 0) * 100)::numeric, 2)   AS avg_peak,
            MODE() WITHIN GROUP (ORDER BY sentiment_key)                                         AS main_pattern,
            MAX(detected_at)                                                                      AS last_signal_at
          FROM tracked_signals
          WHERE status IN ('profit', 'stop_loss', 'cancelled', 'breakeven')
          GROUP BY symbol
          ORDER BY total DESC
        `
    );

    const byToken = (tokenRows.rows as Record<string, unknown>[]).map(r => {
      const tok_profit = Number(r.profit)    || 0;
      const tok_stop   = Number(r.stop_loss) || 0;
      const tok_wrDenom = tok_profit + tok_stop;
      return {
        symbol:        r.symbol as string,
        total:         Number(r.total),
        profit:        tok_profit,
        stopLoss:      tok_stop,
        cancelled:     Number(r.cancelled) || 0,
        winRate:       tok_wrDenom > 0 ? Math.round((tok_profit / tok_wrDenom) * 100) : null,
        avgPnl:        r.avg_pnl  != null ? Number(r.avg_pnl)  : null,
        bestPnl:       r.best_pnl != null ? Number(r.best_pnl) : null,
        avgPeak:       r.avg_peak != null ? Number(r.avg_peak) : null,
        mainPattern:   (r.main_pattern as string) ?? null,
        lastSignalAt:  r.last_signal_at != null ? String(r.last_signal_at) : null,
      };
    });

    // 5. Recent closed trades (last 50) with entry conditions
    const tradeRows = await db.execute(
      fromDate
        ? sql`
          SELECT
            ts.id, ts.symbol, ts.status, ts.sentiment_key,
            ts.entry_price, ts.exit_price,
            ROUND(ts.pnl_percent::numeric, 2)                                               AS pnl_percent,
            ROUND(((ts.peak_price - ts.entry_price)/NULLIF(ts.entry_price,0)*100)::numeric, 2) AS peak_pct,
            ts.detected_at, ts.closed_at,
            ROUND(EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM (COALESCE(ts.closed_at, NOW()) - ts.detected_at))/3600, 1) AS hours_held,
            s.oi_change_percent,
            s.cumulative_oi_change,
            ROUND((s.volume_24h / NULLIF(s.avg_volume, 0))::numeric, 1) AS vol_mult,
            s.alpha_score,
            s.price_change_percent AS px_24h
          FROM tracked_signals ts
          LEFT JOIN signals s ON ts.signal_id = s.id
          WHERE ts.status IN ('profit', 'stop_loss', 'cancelled', 'breakeven')
            AND ts.closed_at >= ${fromDate}
          ORDER BY ts.closed_at DESC
          LIMIT 50
        `
        : sql`
          SELECT
            ts.id, ts.symbol, ts.status, ts.sentiment_key,
            ts.entry_price, ts.exit_price,
            ROUND(ts.pnl_percent::numeric, 2)                                               AS pnl_percent,
            ROUND(((ts.peak_price - ts.entry_price)/NULLIF(ts.entry_price,0)*100)::numeric, 2) AS peak_pct,
            ts.detected_at, ts.closed_at,
            ROUND(EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM (COALESCE(ts.closed_at, NOW()) - ts.detected_at))/3600, 1) AS hours_held,
            s.oi_change_percent,
            s.cumulative_oi_change,
            ROUND((s.volume_24h / NULLIF(s.avg_volume, 0))::numeric, 1) AS vol_mult,
            s.alpha_score,
            s.price_change_percent AS px_24h
          FROM tracked_signals ts
          LEFT JOIN signals s ON ts.signal_id = s.id
          WHERE ts.status IN ('profit', 'stop_loss', 'cancelled', 'breakeven')
          ORDER BY ts.closed_at DESC NULLS LAST
          LIMIT 50
        `
    );

    const trades = (tradeRows.rows as Record<string, unknown>[]).map(r => ({
      id:           r.id,
      symbol:       r.symbol,
      status:       r.status,
      sentimentKey: r.sentiment_key,
      entryPrice:   r.entry_price   != null ? Number(r.entry_price)   : null,
      exitPrice:    r.exit_price    != null ? Number(r.exit_price)    : null,
      pnlPercent:   r.pnl_percent   != null ? Number(r.pnl_percent)   : null,
      peakPct:      r.peak_pct      != null ? Number(r.peak_pct)      : null,
      detectedAt:   r.detected_at,
      closedAt:     r.closed_at     ?? null,
      hoursHeld:    r.hours_held    != null ? Number(r.hours_held)    : null,
      oiChangePct:  r.oi_change_percent   != null ? Number(r.oi_change_percent)   : null,
      cumulativeOi: r.cumulative_oi_change != null ? Number(r.cumulative_oi_change) : null,
      volMult:      r.vol_mult      != null ? Number(r.vol_mult)      : null,
      alphaScore:   r.alpha_score   != null ? Number(r.alpha_score)   : null,
      px24h:        r.px_24h        != null ? Number(r.px_24h)        : null,
    }));

    res.json({
      summary: {
        total, profit, stopLoss, cancelled, breakeven, buStop, active, winRate, avgProfit, avgLoss,
        bestTrade:  bestRows[0]  ? { symbol: bestRows[0].symbol,  pnl: Number(bestRows[0].pnl),  sentimentKey: bestRows[0].sentimentKey  ?? null } : null,
        worstTrade: worstRows[0] ? { symbol: worstRows[0].symbol, pnl: Number(worstRows[0].pnl), sentimentKey: worstRows[0].sentimentKey ?? null } : null,
      },
      byPattern,
      byToken,
      trades,
    });
  } catch (err) {
    req.log.error({ err }, "Failed to fetch journal");
    res.status(500).json({ error: "Failed to fetch journal" });
  }
});

// GET /api/tracker/monthly-stats — stats grouped by calendar month
router.get("/tracker/monthly-stats", async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const rows = await db.execute(sql`
      SELECT
        TO_CHAR(detected_at, 'YYYY-MM') AS month,
        COUNT(*)::int                                                                     AS total,
        COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE status = 'profit')::int                                   AS profit,
        COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE status = 'stop_loss')::int                                AS stop_loss,
        COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE status = 'cancelled')::int                                AS cancelled,
        COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE status = 'breakeven')::int                                AS breakeven,
        COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE status = 'bu_stop')::int                                  AS bu_stop,
        ROUND(AVG(pnl_percent) FILTER (WHERE status = 'profit')::numeric, 2)             AS avg_profit,
        ROUND(AVG(pnl_percent) FILTER (WHERE status = 'stop_loss')::numeric, 2)          AS avg_loss,
        ROUND(AVG((peak_price - entry_price) / NULLIF(entry_price, 0) * 100)
              FILTER (WHERE status IN ('profit','stop_loss'))::numeric, 2)               AS avg_peak,
        MAX(pnl_percent) FILTER (WHERE status = 'profit')                                AS best_pnl,
        MIN(pnl_percent) FILTER (WHERE status = 'stop_loss')                             AS worst_pnl
      FROM tracked_signals
      WHERE status IN ('profit','stop_loss','cancelled','breakeven','bu_stop')
        AND sentiment_key != 'whale_watch_spot'
      GROUP BY TO_CHAR(detected_at, 'YYYY-MM')
      ORDER BY month DESC
    `);

    const months = (rows.rows as Record<string, unknown>[]).map(r => {
      const profit   = Number(r.profit   ?? 0);
      const stopLoss = Number(r.stop_loss ?? 0);
      const buStopM  = Number(r.bu_stop  ?? 0);
      const closed   = profit + stopLoss + Number(r.breakeven ?? 0) + buStopM;
      const winRateDenom = profit + buStopM + stopLoss;
      const winRate  = winRateDenom > 0 ? Math.round(((profit + buStopM) / winRateDenom) * 100) : null;

      const [year, mon] = String(r.month).split("-");
      const monthLabel = new Date(Number(year), Number(mon) - 1, 1)
        .toLocaleDateString("ru-RU", { month: "long", year: "numeric" });

      return {
        month:     r.month    as string,
        label:     monthLabel,
        total:     Number(r.total     ?? 0),
        profit,
        stopLoss,
        cancelled: Number(r.cancelled ?? 0),
        breakeven: Number(r.breakeven ?? 0),
        winRate,
        avgProfit: r.avg_profit != null ? Number(r.avg_profit) : null,
        avgLoss:   r.avg_loss   != null ? Number(r.avg_loss)   : null,
        avgPeak:   r.avg_peak   != null ? Number(r.avg_peak)   : null,
        bestPnl:   r.best_pnl   != null ? Number(r.best_pnl)   : null,
        worstPnl:  r.worst_pnl  != null ? Number(r.worst_pnl)  : null,
      };
    });

    res.json({ months });
  } catch (err) {
    req.log.error({ err }, "Failed to fetch monthly stats");
    res.status(500).json({ error: "Failed to fetch monthly stats" });
  }
});

// GET /api/tracker/stats — summary stats (all-time + current month)
router.get("/tracker/stats", async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const monthStart = new Date();
    monthStart.setDate(1);
    monthStart.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0);

    const [rows, monthRows] = await Promise.all([
      db.select({
        status: trackedSignalsTable.status,
        count: sql<number>`count(*)::int`,
        avgPnl: sql<number>`round(avg(pnl_percent)::numeric, 2)`,
      }).from(trackedSignalsTable).groupBy(trackedSignalsTable.status),

      db.select({
        status: trackedSignalsTable.status,
        count: sql<number>`count(*)::int`,
        avgPnl: sql<number>`round(avg(pnl_percent)::numeric, 2)`,
      }).from(trackedSignalsTable)
        .where(gte(trackedSignalsTable.closedAt, monthStart))
        .groupBy(trackedSignalsTable.status),
    ]);

    const stats: Record<string, { count: number; avgPnl: number | null }> = {};
    for (const row of rows) stats[row.status] = { count: row.count, avgPnl: row.avgPnl };

    const monthStats: Record<string, { count: number; avgPnl: number | null }> = {};
    for (const row of monthRows) monthStats[row.status] = { count: row.count, avgPnl: row.avgPnl };

    const total = rows.reduce((s, r) => s + r.count, 0);
    const profits  = stats["profit"]?.count   ?? 0;
    const buStops  = stats["bu_stop"]?.count  ?? 0;
    const stops    = stats["stop_loss"]?.count ?? 0;
    // bu_stop считается выигрышем (защищённая позиция) — единая формула с /journal и Telegram дайджестом
    const winRate  = (profits + buStops + stops) > 0 ? Math.round(((profits + buStops) / (profits + buStops + stops)) * 100) : null;
    const avgProfit = stats["profit"]?.avgPnl != null ? Number(stats["profit"].avgPnl) : null;
    const avgLoss   = stats["stop_loss"]?.avgPnl != null ? Number(stats["stop_loss"].avgPnl) : null;

    // Month-only derived stats
    const mProfits = monthStats["profit"]?.count  ?? 0;
    const mBuStops = monthStats["bu_stop"]?.count ?? 0;
    const mStops   = monthStats["stop_loss"]?.count ?? 0;
    const monthWinRate  = (mProfits + mBuStops + mStops) > 0 ? Math.round(((mProfits + mBuStops) / (mProfits + mBuStops + mStops)) * 100) : null;
    const monthAvgProfit = monthStats["profit"]?.avgPnl != null ? Number(monthStats["profit"].avgPnl) : null;
    const monthAvgLoss   = monthStats["stop_loss"]?.avgPnl != null ? Number(monthStats["stop_loss"].avgPnl) : null;

    res.json({ stats, total, winRate, avgProfit, avgLoss, monthStats, monthWinRate, monthAvgProfit, monthAvgLoss });
  } catch (err) {
    res.status(500).json({ error: "Failed to fetch tracker stats" });
  }
});

// POST /api/tracker/import — bulk-insert tracked positions (migration/admin tool)
// Body: { positions: Array<{ symbol, sentimentKey, isShort?, entryPrice, detectedAt, status? }> }
router.post("/tracker/import", async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const { positions } = req.body as {
      positions: Array<{
        symbol: string;
        sentimentKey: string;
        isShort?: boolean;
        entryPrice: number;
        detectedAt: string;
        status?: string;
      }>;
    };
    if (!Array.isArray(positions) || positions.length === 0) {
      res.status(400).json({ error: "positions array required" });
      return;
    }
    const now = new Date();
    const inserted: string[] = [];
    const skipped: string[] = [];
    for (const p of positions) {
      // Skip if already open for this symbol
      const existing = await db.select({ id: trackedSignalsTable.id })
        .from(trackedSignalsTable)
        .where(and(
          eq(trackedSignalsTable.symbol, p.symbol),
          inArray(trackedSignalsTable.status, ["active", "in_run", "accumulation", "re_entry"])
        ))
        .limit(1);
      if (existing.length > 0) { skipped.push(p.symbol); continue; }
      await db.insert(trackedSignalsTable).values({
        symbol: p.symbol,
        signalType: "strict_alpha",
        sentimentKey: p.sentimentKey,
        isShort: p.isShort ?? false,
        entryPrice: p.entryPrice,
        status: (p.status ?? "active") as any,
        detectedAt: new Date(p.detectedAt),
        updatedAt: now,
      });
      inserted.push(p.symbol);
    }
    req.log.info({ inserted, skipped }, "tracker/import: positions imported");
    res.json({ ok: true, inserted, skipped });
  } catch (err) {
    req.log.error({ err }, "tracker/import failed");
    res.status(500).json({ error: "Import failed" });
  }
});
