// ── POST /api/tracker/spot-audit ──────────────────────────────────────────────
// Прогоняет все открытые сигналы через спот-фильтр (% от MCap).
// Отменяет те, где данные есть и объём ниже порога.
// Отправляет сводку в Telegram и возвращает JSON с результатами.
router.post("/tracker/spot-audit", async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const OPEN_STATUSES = ["active", "in_run", "accumulation", "re_entry"] as const;
    // Спот-объём >= MIN_PCT * MCap — только процентный порог, без фиксированной суммы.
    // Нет MCap данных → блок.
    const MIN_PCT_ALPHA  = 0.0005; // 0.05% MCap — стандартные паттерны
    const MIN_PCT_WHALE  = 0.003;  // 0.30% MCap — whale_futures / whale_watch

    // 1. Все открытые позиции
    const open = await db.select()
      .from(trackedSignalsTable)
      .where(inArray(trackedSignalsTable.status, [...OPEN_STATUSES]));

    if (open.length === 0) {
      await adminBroadcast(
        `🔍 <b>Аудит спот-фильтра</b>\n\nОткрытых позиций нет — нечего проверять.`
      );
      res.json({ ok: true, total: 0, cancelled: 0, passed: 0, noData: 0 });
      return;
    }

    // 2. Получаем текущие цены для P&L
    const priceMap = new Map<string, number>();
    try {
      const tickers = await getFutureTickers() as { symbol: string; lastPrice: string }[];
      for (const t of tickers) priceMap.set(t.symbol, parseFloat(t.lastPrice) || 0);
    } catch { /* skip */ }

    // 3. Спот-объёмы — параллельно (BingX, без rate limit проблем)
    //    MCap — одним батч-запросом к CoinGecko (экономим rate limit)
    type AuditRow = {
      sig:          TrackedSignal;
      spotVol24h:   number | null;
      marketCapUsd: number | null;
      spotPct:      number | null;
      minPct:       number;
      verdict:      "cancel" | "pass" | "no_data";
    };

    const symbols = open.map(s => s.symbol);

    // Параллельно: все spot vol + один батч mcap запрос
    const [spotVolMap, mcapMap] = await Promise.all([
      Promise.all(symbols.map(sym =>
        getCoinglassSpotVolume24h(sym).catch(() => null)
          .then(vol => [sym, vol] as [string, number | null])
      )).then(entries => new Map(entries)),
      batchFetchMarketCap(symbols),
    ]);

    const rows: AuditRow[] = open.map(sig => {
      const minPct       = sig.sentimentKey === "whale_futures" ? MIN_PCT_WHALE : MIN_PCT_ALPHA;
      const spotVol24h   = spotVolMap.get(sig.symbol) ?? null;
      const mcapData     = mcapMap.get(sig.symbol) ?? null;
      const marketCapUsd = mcapData?.mcap ?? null;
      const spotPct      = (spotVol24h != null && marketCapUsd != null && marketCapUsd > 0)
        ? (spotVol24h / marketCapUsd) * 100
        : null;

      // Только процентный порог от MCap — нет MCap данных → no_data (не отменяем).
      let verdict: AuditRow["verdict"] = "no_data";
      if (spotVol24h !== null && marketCapUsd !== null) {
        const pctOk = spotPct !== null && spotPct >= (minPct * 100);
        verdict = pctOk ? "pass" : "cancel";
      }

      return { sig, spotVol24h, marketCapUsd, spotPct, minPct, verdict };
    });

    // 4. Отмена: массовый UPDATE
    const toCancel = rows.filter(r => r.verdict === "cancel");
    if (toCancel.length > 0) {
      await db.update(trackedSignalsTable)
        .set({ status: "cancelled", cancelReason: "spot_audit_low_vol", closedAt: new Date(), updatedAt: new Date() })
        .where(inArray(trackedSignalsTable.id, toCancel.map(r => r.sig.id)));
      logger.info(
        { cancelled: toCancel.map(r => r.sig.symbol) },
        "spot-audit: принудительно отменены сигналы с низким спот-объёмом"
      );
    }

    // 5. Telegram сводка
    const now = new Date();
    const moscowTime = now.toLocaleString("ru-RU", {
      timeZone: "Europe/Moscow",
      day: "2-digit", month: "long", hour: "2-digit", minute: "2-digit",
    });

    function fmtP(p: number): string {
      if (p >= 1) return `$${p.toFixed(3)}`;
      if (p >= 0.01) return `$${p.toFixed(4)}`;
      return `$${p.toFixed(5)}`;
    }
    function computePnl(sig: TrackedSignal, curPrice: number): number {
      if (!sig.entryPrice || sig.entryPrice === 0) return 0;
      const diff = curPrice - sig.entryPrice;
      return (sig.isShort ? -diff : diff) / sig.entryPrice * 100;
    }

    const lines: string[] = [
      `🔍 <b>Аудит спот-фильтра</b> — <i>${moscowTime} МСК</i>`,
      ``,
      `Проверено позиций: <b>${open.length}</b>`,
      ``,
    ];

    // Отменённые
    if (toCancel.length > 0) {
      lines.push(`❌ <b>Закрыты принудительно — ${toCancel.length}</b>`);
      for (const r of toCancel) {
        const coin    = r.sig.symbol.replace("-USDT", "");
        const pctStr  = r.spotPct != null ? `${r.spotPct.toFixed(1)}% MCap` : "?% MCap";
        const reqStr  = `${(r.minPct * 100).toFixed(0)}%`;
        const curPrice = priceMap.get(r.sig.symbol);
        const pnl      = curPrice ? computePnl(r.sig, curPrice) : null;
        const pnlStr   = pnl != null ? ` | P&L: ${pnl >= 0 ? "+" : ""}${pnl.toFixed(1)}%` : "";
        lines.push(`  • <b>${coin}</b> — спот ${pctStr} (нужно ≥${reqStr})${pnlStr}`);
      }
      lines.push(``);
    }

    // Прошли фильтр
    const passed = rows.filter(r => r.verdict === "pass");
    if (passed.length > 0) {
      lines.push(`✅ <b>Прошли фильтр — ${passed.length}</b>`);
      for (const r of passed) {
        const coin     = r.sig.symbol.replace("-USDT", "");
        const pctStr   = r.spotPct != null ? `${r.spotPct.toFixed(1)}% MCap` : "";
        const curPrice = priceMap.get(r.sig.symbol);
        const pnl      = curPrice ? computePnl(r.sig, curPrice) : null;
        const pnlStr   = pnl != null ? ` | ${pnl >= 0 ? "+" : ""}${pnl.toFixed(1)}%` : "";
        const priceStr = curPrice ? ` | ${fmtP(r.sig.entryPrice ?? 0)} → ${fmtP(curPrice)}` : "";
        lines.push(`  • <b>${coin}</b> — ${pctStr}${priceStr}${pnlStr}`);
      }
      lines.push(``);
    }

    // Нет данных (не блокировали — но сообщаем)
    const noData = rows.filter(r => r.verdict === "no_data");
    if (noData.length > 0) {
      lines.push(`⚠️ <b>Нет данных MCap — ${noData.length} (не тронуты)</b>`);
      for (const r of noData) {
        const coin = r.sig.symbol.replace("-USDT", "");
        lines.push(`  • <b>${coin}</b>`);
      }
      lines.push(``);
    }

    lines.push(`📋 Итог: закрыто <b>${toCancel.length}</b>, активно <b>${passed.length + noData.length}</b>`);

    await adminBroadcast(lines.join("\n"));

    // После аудита — сразу отправить актуальный список позиций
    try {
      const posText = await buildPositionsStatusText();
      await adminBroadcast(posText);
    } catch (err) {
      logger.warn({ err }, "spot-audit: не удалось отправить список позиций после аудита");
    }

    res.json({
      ok: true,
      total: open.length,
      cancelled: toCancel.length,
      passed: passed.length,
      noData: noData.length,
      cancelledSymbols: toCancel.map(r => r.sig.symbol),
      passedSymbols:    passed.map(r => r.sig.symbol),
      noDataSymbols:    noData.map(r => r.sig.symbol),
    });
  } catch (err) {
    logger.error({ err }, "spot-audit: ошибка");
    res.status(500).json({ error: "Spot audit failed" });
  }
});

// ── POST /api/tracker/backfill-whale-seeds ────────────────────────────────────
// Разовый бэкфилл: засевает все исторические отменённые сигналы с OI ≥3%
// которые ещё не имеют активного сида в signal_whale_seeds.
router.post("/tracker/backfill-whale-seeds", async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const rows = await db.execute(sql`
      SELECT
        ts.id                   AS tracked_id,
        ts.symbol,
        ts.signal_id,
        ts.sentiment_key,
        ts.entry_price,
        ts.closed_at,
        COALESCE(s.cumulative_oi_change, s.oi_change_percent, 0) AS oi_magnitude
      FROM tracked_signals ts
      LEFT JOIN signals s ON s.id = ts.signal_id
      WHERE ts.status = 'cancelled'
        AND ts.signal_id IS NOT NULL
        AND COALESCE(s.cumulative_oi_change, s.oi_change_percent, 0) >= 3.0
        AND NOT EXISTS (
          SELECT 1 FROM signal_whale_seeds w
          WHERE w.symbol = ts.symbol AND w.status = 'watching'
        )
      ORDER BY ts.closed_at DESC
    `);

    const candidates = rows.rows as {
      tracked_id: number; symbol: string; signal_id: number;
      sentiment_key: string | null; entry_price: number;
      closed_at: string; oi_magnitude: number;
    }[];

    let seeded = 0;
    for (const c of candidates) {
      const closedAt = new Date(c.closed_at);
      const expiresAt = new Date(closedAt.getTime() + 90 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000);
      if (expiresAt < new Date()) continue; // уже истёк бы

      // повторная проверка дедупликации (для гонок)
      const existing = await db.select({ id: signalWhaleSeedsTable.id })
        .from(signalWhaleSeedsTable)
        .where(and(
          eq(signalWhaleSeedsTable.symbol, c.symbol),
          eq(signalWhaleSeedsTable.status, "watching"),
        )).limit(1);
      if (existing.length > 0) continue;

      await db.insert(signalWhaleSeedsTable).values({
        symbol:          c.symbol,
        trackedSignalId: c.tracked_id,
        signalId:        c.signal_id,
        sentimentKey:    c.sentiment_key ?? null,
        oiAtSeed:        c.oi_magnitude,
        priceAtSeed:     c.entry_price,
        seededAt:        closedAt,
        expiresAt,
      });
      seeded++;
    }

    req.log.info({ total: candidates.length, seeded }, "🌱 Whale backfill complete");
    res.json({ ok: true, candidates: candidates.length, seeded });
  } catch (err) {
    req.log.error({ err }, "Whale backfill failed");
    res.status(500).json({ error: "Backfill failed" });
  }
});

// GET /api/tracker/whale-seeds — cancelled signals seeded for whale watch
router.get("/tracker/whale-seeds", async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const rows = await db.execute(sql`
      SELECT
        s.id, s.symbol, s.sentiment_key,
        ROUND(s.oi_at_seed::numeric, 2)    AS oi_at_seed,
        s.price_at_seed,
        s.seeded_at, s.expires_at, s.status,
        ROUND(s.oi_growth_pct::numeric, 2) AS oi_growth_pct,
        s.last_checked_at,
        ROUND(EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM (NOW() - s.seeded_at))/86400, 1) AS days_since_seeded
      FROM signal_whale_seeds s
      WHERE s.status = 'watching'
      ORDER BY s.seeded_at DESC
      LIMIT 100
    `);

    const seeds = (rows.rows as Record<string, unknown>[]).map(r => ({
      id:              r.id,
      symbol:          r.symbol,
      sentimentKey:    r.sentiment_key   ?? null,
      oiAtSeed:        r.oi_at_seed      != null ? Number(r.oi_at_seed)      : null,
      priceAtSeed:     r.price_at_seed   != null ? Number(r.price_at_seed)   : null,
      seededAt:        r.seeded_at,
      expiresAt:       r.expires_at,
      status:          r.status,
      oiGrowthPct:     r.oi_growth_pct   != null ? Number(r.oi_growth_pct)   : null,
      lastCheckedAt:   r.last_checked_at ?? null,
      daysSinceSeeded: r.days_since_seeded != null ? Number(r.days_since_seeded) : 0,
    }));

    res.json({ seeds, total: seeds.length });
  } catch (err) {
    req.log.error({ err }, "Failed to fetch whale seeds");
    res.status(500).json({ error: "Failed to fetch whale seeds" });
  }
});

// GET /api/tracker/whale-watch-spot-stats — spot signal performance stats
router.get("/tracker/whale-watch-spot-stats", async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const rows = await db.execute(sql`
      SELECT
        id, symbol,
        detected_at, fired_at,
        price_at_detection,
        ROUND(price_24h_pct::numeric, 2)   AS price_24h_pct,
        ROUND(price_48h_pct::numeric, 2)   AS price_48h_pct,
        ROUND(price_7d_max_pct::numeric, 2) AS price_7d_max_pct
      FROM whale_futures_watchlist
      WHERE type = 'spot' AND status = 'fired'
      ORDER BY fired_at DESC
      LIMIT 100
    `);

    const signals = (rows.rows as Record<string, unknown>[]).map(r => ({
      id:               r.id,
      symbol:           r.symbol as string,
      detectedAt:       r.detected_at,
      firedAt:          r.fired_at,
      priceAtDetection: r.price_at_detection != null ? Number(r.price_at_detection) : null,
      price24hPct:      r.price_24h_pct      != null ? Number(r.price_24h_pct)      : null,
      price48hPct:      r.price_48h_pct      != null ? Number(r.price_48h_pct)      : null,
      price7dMaxPct:    r.price_7d_max_pct   != null ? Number(r.price_7d_max_pct)   : null,
    }));

    // Aggregate stats (only from signals with at least 24h data)
    const withData = signals.filter(s => s.price24hPct != null);
    const total = signals.length;
    const withStats = withData.length;
    const WIN_THRESHOLD = 5; // 5% = win
    const wins24h = withData.filter(s => (s.price24hPct ?? 0) >= WIN_THRESHOLD).length;
    const wins48h = withData.filter(s => (s.price48hPct ?? 0) >= WIN_THRESHOLD).length;
    const wins7d  = withData.filter(s => (s.price7dMaxPct ?? 0) >= WIN_THRESHOLD).length;

    const avg = (arr: number[]) => arr.length ? arr.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / arr.length : null;
    const avg24h    = avg(withData.map(s => s.price24hPct!));
    const avg48h    = avg(withData.filter(s => s.price48hPct != null).map(s => s.price48hPct!));
    const avg7dMax  = avg(withData.filter(s => s.price7dMaxPct != null).map(s => s.price7dMaxPct!));

    res.json({
      total,
      withStats,
      winRate24h:  withStats > 0 ? Math.round((wins24h / withStats) * 100) : null,
      winRate48h:  withStats > 0 ? Math.round((wins48h / withStats) * 100) : null,
      winRate7d:   withStats > 0 ? Math.round((wins7d  / withStats) * 100) : null,
      avg24hPct:   avg24h   != null ? Number(avg24h.toFixed(2))   : null,
      avg48hPct:   avg48h   != null ? Number(avg48h.toFixed(2))   : null,
      avg7dMaxPct: avg7dMax != null ? Number(avg7dMax.toFixed(2)) : null,
      signals,
    });
  } catch (err) {
    req.log.error({ err }, "Failed to fetch whale-watch-spot-stats");
    res.status(500).json({ error: "Failed to fetch whale-watch-spot-stats" });
  }
});

// Немедленно отправляет актуальный список открытых позиций в Telegram.
router.post("/tracker/send-positions", async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const text = await buildPositionsStatusText();
    await adminBroadcast(text);
    res.json({ ok: true });
  } catch (err) {
    logger.error({ err }, "send-positions: ошибка");
    res.status(500).json({ error: "Send positions failed" });
  }
});

// GET /api/tracker/lateness-analysis — signal timing & pattern analysis (last 24h + 30d context)
router.get("/tracker/lateness-analysis", async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const [summaryRows, priceBucketRows, patternRows, oiBucketRows] = await Promise.all([

      db.execute(sql`
        SELECT
          COUNT(*)::int                                                                     AS total,
          COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE status IN ('profit','bu_stop'))::int                      AS profit,
          COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE status = 'stop_loss')::int                                AS stop_loss,
          COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE status = 'cancelled')::int                                AS cancelled,
          COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE status = 'breakeven')::int                                AS breakeven,
          ROUND(AVG(pnl_percent) FILTER (WHERE status IN ('profit','bu_stop'))::numeric,1) AS avg_profit_pnl,
          ROUND(AVG(pnl_percent) FILTER (WHERE status = 'stop_loss')::numeric, 1)          AS avg_loss_pnl,
          ROUND(MAX(pnl_percent) FILTER (WHERE status IN ('profit','bu_stop'))::numeric, 1) AS best_pnl
        FROM tracked_signals
        WHERE detected_at >= NOW() - INTERVAL '1 day'
          AND status NOT IN ('active','in_run','accumulation','re_entry')
          AND COALESCE(sentiment_key,'') NOT IN ('whale_watch_spot')
      `),

      db.execute(sql`
        SELECT
          CASE
            WHEN COALESCE(ts.price_24h_at_fire, s.price_change_percent) IS NULL THEN 'unknown'
            WHEN ABS(COALESCE(ts.price_24h_at_fire, s.price_change_percent)) < 3  THEN '0–3%'
            WHEN ABS(COALESCE(ts.price_24h_at_fire, s.price_change_percent)) < 7  THEN '3–7%'
            WHEN ABS(COALESCE(ts.price_24h_at_fire, s.price_change_percent)) < 15 THEN '7–15%'
            ELSE '15%+'
          END AS bucket,
          CASE
            WHEN COALESCE(ts.price_24h_at_fire, s.price_change_percent) IS NULL THEN 99
            WHEN ABS(COALESCE(ts.price_24h_at_fire, s.price_change_percent)) < 3  THEN 0
            WHEN ABS(COALESCE(ts.price_24h_at_fire, s.price_change_percent)) < 7  THEN 1
            WHEN ABS(COALESCE(ts.price_24h_at_fire, s.price_change_percent)) < 15 THEN 2
            ELSE 3
          END AS bucket_order,
          COUNT(*)::int                                                                          AS total,
          COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE ts.status IN ('profit','bu_stop'))::int                        AS profit,
          COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE ts.status = 'stop_loss')::int                                  AS stop_loss,
          COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE ts.status = 'cancelled')::int                                  AS cancelled,
          ROUND(AVG(ts.pnl_percent) FILTER (WHERE ts.status IN ('profit','bu_stop'))::numeric,1) AS avg_profit_pnl,
          ROUND(AVG(ts.pnl_percent) FILTER (WHERE ts.status = 'stop_loss')::numeric, 1)          AS avg_loss_pnl,
          ROUND(AVG((ts.peak_price - ts.entry_price) / NULLIF(ts.entry_price,0) * 100)
            FILTER (WHERE ts.status IN ('profit','stop_loss','bu_stop'))::numeric, 1)            AS avg_peak_pct
        FROM tracked_signals ts
        LEFT JOIN signals s ON s.id = ts.signal_id
        WHERE ts.detected_at >= NOW() - INTERVAL '1 day'
          AND ts.status NOT IN ('active','in_run','accumulation','re_entry')
          AND COALESCE(ts.sentiment_key,'') NOT IN ('whale_watch_spot')
        GROUP BY bucket, bucket_order
        ORDER BY bucket_order
      `),

      db.execute(sql`
        SELECT
          COALESCE(ts.sentiment_key,'oi_watch_auto')                                             AS pattern,
          COUNT(*)::int                                                                           AS total,
          COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE ts.status IN ('profit','bu_stop'))::int                         AS profit,
          COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE ts.status = 'stop_loss')::int                                   AS stop_loss,
          COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE ts.status = 'cancelled')::int                                   AS cancelled,
          ROUND(AVG(ts.pnl_percent) FILTER (WHERE ts.status IN ('profit','bu_stop'))::numeric,1) AS avg_profit_pnl,
          ROUND(AVG(ts.pnl_percent) FILTER (WHERE ts.status = 'stop_loss')::numeric, 1)          AS avg_loss_pnl,
          ROUND(AVG((ts.peak_price - ts.entry_price) / NULLIF(ts.entry_price,0) * 100)
            FILTER (WHERE ts.status IN ('profit','stop_loss','bu_stop'))::numeric, 1)            AS avg_peak_pct,
          ROUND(AVG(COALESCE(ts.price_24h_at_fire, s.price_change_percent))::numeric, 1)        AS avg_price_at_fire,
          ROUND(AVG(s.oi_change_percent)::numeric, 1)                                            AS avg_oi_at_fire,
          ROUND(AVG(ts.prescan_cycles)::numeric, 1)                                              AS avg_prescan_cycles
        FROM tracked_signals ts
        LEFT JOIN signals s ON s.id = ts.signal_id
        WHERE ts.detected_at >= NOW() - INTERVAL '1 day'
          AND ts.status NOT IN ('active','in_run','accumulation','re_entry')
          AND COALESCE(ts.sentiment_key,'') NOT IN ('whale_watch_spot')
        GROUP BY ts.sentiment_key
        HAVING COUNT(*) >= 1
        ORDER BY COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE ts.status IN ('profit','bu_stop')) DESC, COUNT(*) DESC
      `),

      db.execute(sql`
        SELECT
          CASE
            WHEN s.oi_change_percent IS NULL   THEN 'unknown'
            WHEN s.oi_change_percent < 5       THEN '<5%'
            WHEN s.oi_change_percent < 15      THEN '5–15%'
            WHEN s.oi_change_percent < 30      THEN '15–30%'
            ELSE '30%+'
          END AS bucket,
          CASE
            WHEN s.oi_change_percent IS NULL   THEN 99
            WHEN s.oi_change_percent < 5       THEN 0
            WHEN s.oi_change_percent < 15      THEN 1
            WHEN s.oi_change_percent < 30      THEN 2
            ELSE 3
          END AS bucket_order,
          COUNT(*)::int                                                                           AS total,
          COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE ts.status IN ('profit','bu_stop'))::int                         AS profit,
          COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE ts.status = 'stop_loss')::int                                   AS stop_loss,
          ROUND(AVG(ts.pnl_percent) FILTER (WHERE ts.status IN ('profit','bu_stop'))::numeric,1) AS avg_profit_pnl
        FROM tracked_signals ts
        LEFT JOIN signals s ON s.id = ts.signal_id
        WHERE ts.detected_at >= NOW() - INTERVAL '1 day'
          AND ts.status NOT IN ('active','in_run','accumulation','re_entry')
          AND COALESCE(ts.sentiment_key,'') NOT IN ('whale_watch_spot')
        GROUP BY bucket, bucket_order
        ORDER BY bucket_order
      `),
    ]);

    const s = (summaryRows.rows[0] ?? {}) as Record<string, unknown>;
    const profit   = Number(s.profit   ?? 0);
    const stopLoss = Number(s.stop_loss ?? 0);
    const closed   = profit + stopLoss + Number(s.breakeven ?? 0);
    const winRate  = (profit + stopLoss) > 0 ? Math.round((profit / (profit + stopLoss)) * 100) : null;

    const mapBucket = (rows: Record<string, unknown>[]) =>
      rows.map(r => {
        const t = Number(r.total  ?? 0);
        const p = Number(r.profit ?? 0);
        const sl= Number(r.stop_loss ?? 0);
        return {
          bucket:       String(r.bucket ?? ""),
          total:        t,
          profit:       p,
          stopLoss:     sl,
          cancelled:    Number(r.cancelled ?? 0),
          winRate:      (p + sl) > 0 ? Math.round((p / (p + sl)) * 100) : null,
          avgProfitPnl: r.avg_profit_pnl != null ? Number(r.avg_profit_pnl) : null,
          avgLossPnl:   r.avg_loss_pnl   != null ? Number(r.avg_loss_pnl)   : null,
          avgPeakPct:   r.avg_peak_pct   != null ? Number(r.avg_peak_pct)   : null,
        };
      });

    const priceBuckets = mapBucket(priceBucketRows.rows as Record<string, unknown>[]);
    const oiBuckets    = mapBucket(oiBucketRows.rows    as Record<string, unknown>[]);

    const patterns = (patternRows.rows as Record<string, unknown>[]).map(r => {
      const t  = Number(r.total   ?? 0);
      const p  = Number(r.profit  ?? 0);
      const sl = Number(r.stop_loss ?? 0);
      return {
        pattern:           String(r.pattern ?? ""),
        total:             t,
        profit:            p,
        stopLoss:          sl,
        cancelled:         Number(r.cancelled ?? 0),
        winRate:           (p + sl) > 0 ? Math.round((p / (p + sl)) * 100) : null,
        avgProfitPnl:      r.avg_profit_pnl    != null ? Number(r.avg_profit_pnl)    : null,
        avgLossPnl:        r.avg_loss_pnl      != null ? Number(r.avg_loss_pnl)      : null,
        avgPeakPct:        r.avg_peak_pct      != null ? Number(r.avg_peak_pct)      : null,
        avgPriceAtFire:    r.avg_price_at_fire != null ? Number(r.avg_price_at_fire) : null,
        avgOiAtFire:       r.avg_oi_at_fire    != null ? Number(r.avg_oi_at_fire)    : null,
        avgPrescanCycles:  r.avg_prescan_cycles != null ? Number(r.avg_prescan_cycles) : null,
      };
    });

    const totalSignals = Number(s.total ?? 0);
    const withPriceData = priceBuckets.filter(b => b.bucket !== "unknown").reduce((a, b) => a + b.total, 0);

    res.json({
      summary: {
        total:        totalSignals,
        profit,
        stopLoss,
        cancelled:    Number(s.cancelled ?? 0),
        breakeven:    Number(s.breakeven ?? 0),
        closed,
        winRate,
        avgProfitPnl: s.avg_profit_pnl != null ? Number(s.avg_profit_pnl) : null,
        avgLossPnl:   s.avg_loss_pnl   != null ? Number(s.avg_loss_pnl)   : null,
        bestPnl:      s.best_pnl       != null ? Number(s.best_pnl)       : null,
      },
      coverage: {
        totalSignals,
        withPriceData,
        pctWithData: totalSignals > 0 ? Math.round((withPriceData / totalSignals) * 100) : 0,
      },
      priceBuckets,
      oiBuckets,
      patterns,
    });
  } catch (err) {
    req.log.error({ err }, "lateness-analysis: query failed");
    res.status(500).json({ error: "Analysis query failed" });
  }
});

export default router;

import pino from "pino";

    "req.headers.authorization",

    "res.headers['set-cookie']",

FILE: artifacts/api-server/src/lib/apiHealth.ts

/**
 * API Health Monitor — отслеживает сбои внешних API и шлёт Telegram-алерт.
 *
 * Логика:
 * - 401/403 → критичная ошибка (ключ), алерт после 1-й ошибки
 * - 5xx/timeout → временный сбой, алерт после 3 подряд
 * - Восстановление → уведомление о восстановлении
 */

import { logger } from "./logger.js";

export type ApiName = "coinglass" | "coinalyze" | "bingx" | "coingecko" | "coinpaprika";

const API_LABELS: Record<ApiName, string> = {
  coinglass:   "CoinGlass",
  coinalyze:   "Coinalyze",
  bingx:       "BingX",
  coingecko:   "CoinGecko",
  coinpaprika: "CoinPaprika",
};

interface HealthState {
  consecutiveFailures: number;
  alertSent:           boolean;
  lastStatus?:         number;
  lastFailureAt?:      number;
}

const states = new Map<ApiName, HealthState>();

function getState(api: ApiName): HealthState {
  if (!states.has(api)) states.set(api, { consecutiveFailures: 0, alertSent: false });
  return states.get(api)!;
}

/** Вызвать когда API ответил успешно */
export function reportApiSuccess(api: ApiName): void {
  const s = getState(api);
  if (s.consecutiveFailures === 0) return; // всё и так ок

  const wasAlerted = s.alertSent;
  s.consecutiveFailures = 0;
  s.alertSent = false;

  if (wasAlerted) {
    void sendAdminAlert(
      `✅ <b>${API_LABELS[api]} восстановлен</b>\n\nAPI снова отвечает нормально.`
    );
  }
}

/** Вызвать когда API вернул ошибку */
export function reportApiFailure(api: ApiName, statusCode?: number): void {
  const s = getState(api);
  s.consecutiveFailures++;
  s.lastStatus    = statusCode;
  s.lastFailureAt = Date.now();

  // 401/403 — проблема с ключом, бьём тревогу сразу
  const isCritical = statusCode === 401 || statusCode === 403;
  const threshold  = isCritical ? 1 : 3;

  if (s.consecutiveFailures >= threshold && !s.alertSent) {
    s.alertSent = true;
    const label  = API_LABELS[api];
    const code   = statusCode ? ` (HTTP ${statusCode})` : "";
    const reason = isCritical
      ? "🔑 Проблема с API-ключом — возможно сброшен или истёк срок."
      : `Подряд ошибок: ${s.consecutiveFailures}`;

    void sendAdminAlert(
      `⚠️ <b>${label} недоступен${code}</b>\n\n` +
      `${reason}\n\n` +
      `Сигналы работают в ограниченном режиме.`
    );
  }
}

/** Возвращает текущий статус подключения (false = есть последовательные ошибки) */
export function getApiConnected(api: ApiName): boolean {
  return getState(api).consecutiveFailures === 0;
}

async function sendAdminAlert(text: string): Promise<void> {
  const token  = process.env.TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN;
  const chatId = process.env.TELEGRAM_CHAT_ID;
  if (!token || !chatId) return;

  try {
    const res = await fetch(`https://api.telegram.org/bot${token}/sendMessage`, {
      method:  "POST",
      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      body:    JSON.stringify({ chat_id: chatId, text, parse_mode: "HTML" }),
      signal:  AbortSignal.timeout(8000),
    });
    if (res.ok) {
      logger.info({ api: text.slice(0, 40) }, "API health alert sent to admin");
    }
  } catch (err) {
    logger.warn({ err }, "Failed to send API health alert");
  }
}


================================================================
FILE: artifacts/api-server/src/lib/bingx.ts
