/**
 * Whale Accumulation Tracker
 *
 * Сохраняет ежедневные снимки OI + объём для всех активных токенов.
 * Анализирует тренды: если OI стабильно растёт несколько недель при плоской цене —
 * это признак китового накопления.
 *
 * Цикл сбора: 1 раз в сутки (в 00:05 UTC).
 * Анализ: запускается при каждом скане (если данных >= 14 дней).
 */

import { db, oiDailySnapshotsTable } from "@workspace/db";
import { eq, and, gte, sql } from "drizzle-orm";
import type { CoinglassOIResult } from "./coinglass_oi.js";
import { logger } from "./logger.js";

const TODAY_UTC = () => new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10); // "YYYY-MM-DD"

// ── Snapshot collection ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────

/**
 * Сохраняет дневной снимок OI для всех символов из batch CoinGlass.
 * Вызывается один раз в сутки. Дубли (один символ + одна дата) игнорируются.
 */
export async function saveDailyOiSnapshots(
  batchData: Map<string, CoinglassOIResult>,
  priceMap: Map<string, number>,
  volume24hMap: Map<string, number>,
): Promise<{ saved: number; skipped: number }> {
  const date = TODAY_UTC();
  let saved = 0;
  let skipped = 0;

  for (const [symbol, oi] of batchData.entries()) {
    if (!oi.available || oi.oiUsd == null) { skipped++; continue; }

    // Проверяем нет ли уже записи за сегодня
    const existing = await db
      .select({ id: oiDailySnapshotsTable.id })
      .from(oiDailySnapshotsTable)
      .where(and(
        eq(oiDailySnapshotsTable.symbol, symbol),
        eq(oiDailySnapshotsTable.date, date),
      ))
      .limit(1);

    if (existing.length > 0) { skipped++; continue; }

    await db.insert(oiDailySnapshotsTable).values({
      symbol,
      date,
      oiUsd:       oi.oiUsd,
      oiChange24h: oi.oiChange24h ?? null,
      priceUsd:    priceMap.get(symbol) ?? null,
      volume24h:   volume24hMap.get(symbol) ?? null,
    });
    saved++;
  }

  logger.info({ date, saved, skipped }, "🐋 Daily OI snapshots saved");
  return { saved, skipped };
}

// ── Whale analysis ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export interface WhaleAccumResult {
  symbol:          string;
  daysTracked:     number;
  oiGrowthPct:     number;   // OI % change from first to last snapshot
  priceChangePct:  number;   // Price % change over same period
  oiGrowthPerDay:  number;   // Average daily OI growth %
  positiveDays:    number;   // How many days had positive OI change24h
  totalDays:       number;
  consistencyPct:  number;   // positiveDays / totalDays * 100
  avgVolRatio:     number;   // avg volume vs first-week avg (volume building signal)
  isWhaleAccum:    boolean;
  confidence:      "high" | "medium" | "low";
  reason:          string;
}

/**
 * Анализирует исторические снимки для одного символа.
 * Возвращает null если данных недостаточно (< 14 дней).
 */
export async function analyzeWhaleAccumulation(symbol: string): Promise<WhaleAccumResult | null> {
  const cutoff = new Date();
  cutoff.setDate(cutoff.getDate() - 90); // берём до 90 дней

  const snapshots = await db
    .select()
    .from(oiDailySnapshotsTable)
    .where(and(
      eq(oiDailySnapshotsTable.symbol, symbol),
      gte(oiDailySnapshotsTable.takenAt, cutoff),
    ))
    .orderBy(oiDailySnapshotsTable.date);

  if (snapshots.length < 14) return null; // нужно минимум 2 недели

  const first   = snapshots[0];
  const last    = snapshots[snapshots.length - 1];
  const daysTracked = snapshots.length;

  if (!first.oiUsd || !last.oiUsd || first.oiUsd === 0) return null;

  // OI рост от начала к концу периода
  const oiGrowthPct = ((last.oiUsd - first.oiUsd) / first.oiUsd) * 100;

  // Изменение цены за период
  const priceChangePct = (first.priceUsd && last.priceUsd && first.priceUsd > 0)
    ? ((last.priceUsd - first.priceUsd) / first.priceUsd) * 100
    : 0;

  // Средний дневной рост OI
  const oiGrowthPerDay = oiGrowthPct / daysTracked;

  // Сколько дней был положительный oiChange24h (накопление, не распределение)
  const positiveDays = snapshots.filter(s => (s.oiChange24h ?? 0) > 0).length;
  const consistencyPct = (positiveDays / daysTracked) * 100;

  // Сравниваем средний объём первой недели vs последней недели
  const firstWeek = snapshots.slice(0, 7).filter(s => s.volume24h != null);
  const lastWeek  = snapshots.slice(-7).filter(s => s.volume24h != null);
  const firstVolAvg = firstWeek.length > 0
    ? firstWeek.reduce((s, r) => s + (r.volume24h ?? 0), 0) / firstWeek.length : 0;
  const lastVolAvg  = lastWeek.length > 0
    ? lastWeek.reduce((s, r) => s + (r.volume24h ?? 0), 0) / lastWeek.length : 0;
  const avgVolRatio = firstVolAvg > 0 ? lastVolAvg / firstVolAvg : 1;

  // ── Критерии китового накопления ─────────────────────────────────────────
  // 1. OI вырос минимум на 15% за период
  // 2. Цена не выросла сильно (< 15%) — значит накопление ещё не реализовалось
  // 3. Накопление устойчивое: > 55% дней с позитивным OI
  const oiGrowing     = oiGrowthPct >= 15;
  const priceSuppressed = priceChangePct < 15;
  const consistent    = consistencyPct >= 55;
  const volumeBuilding = avgVolRatio >= 1.2; // объём в последнюю неделю на 20% выше первой

  const isWhaleAccum  = oiGrowing && priceSuppressed && consistent;

  // Уверенность
  let confidence: "high" | "medium" | "low" = "low";
  let reason = "";

  if (isWhaleAccum) {
    if (oiGrowthPct >= 40 && consistencyPct >= 65 && volumeBuilding) {
      confidence = "high";
      reason = `OI +${oiGrowthPct.toFixed(0)}% за ${daysTracked}д при цене ${priceChangePct >= 0 ? "+" : ""}${priceChangePct.toFixed(0)}%, объём растёт (${avgVolRatio.toFixed(1)}x)`;
    } else if (oiGrowthPct >= 25 && consistencyPct >= 60) {
      confidence = "medium";
      reason = `OI +${oiGrowthPct.toFixed(0)}% за ${daysTracked}д при цене ${priceChangePct >= 0 ? "+" : ""}${priceChangePct.toFixed(0)}%`;
    } else {
      confidence = "low";
      reason = `Слабые признаки: OI +${oiGrowthPct.toFixed(0)}% за ${daysTracked}д`;
    }
  }

  return {
    symbol, daysTracked, oiGrowthPct, priceChangePct,
    oiGrowthPerDay, positiveDays, totalDays: daysTracked,
    consistencyPct, avgVolRatio, isWhaleAccum, confidence, reason,
  };
}

/**
 * Быстрая проверка: есть ли по символу достаточно данных для whale-анализа?
 */
export async function hasEnoughWhaleData(symbol: string): Promise<boolean> {
  const result = await db
    .select({ cnt: sql<number>`count(*)` })
    .from(oiDailySnapshotsTable)
    .where(eq(oiDailySnapshotsTable.symbol, symbol));
  return (Number(result[0]?.cnt ?? 0)) >= 14;
}

/**
 * Возвращает все символы с активным whale accumulation (HIGH или MEDIUM confidence).
 * Используется для ежедневного отчёта.
 */
export async function getAllWhaleAccumCandidates(): Promise<WhaleAccumResult[]> {
  // Получаем список символов с >= 14 снимками
  const symbolsWithData = await db
    .selectDistinct({ symbol: oiDailySnapshotsTable.symbol })
    .from(oiDailySnapshotsTable)
    .groupBy(oiDailySnapshotsTable.symbol)
    .having(sql`count(*) >= 14`);

  const results: WhaleAccumResult[] = [];
  for (const { symbol } of symbolsWithData) {
    const analysis = await analyzeWhaleAccumulation(symbol);
    if (analysis?.isWhaleAccum && analysis.confidence !== "low") {
      results.push(analysis);
    }
  }

  // Сортируем по силе: сначала высокая уверенность + больший рост OI
  return results.sort((a, b) => {
    if (a.confidence !== b.confidence) {
      return a.confidence === "high" ? -1 : 1;
    }
    return b.oiGrowthPct - a.oiGrowthPct;
  });
}

export interface EarlyWhaleCandidate {
  symbol:        string;
  days:          number;
  oiGrowthPct:   number;
  priceChangePct: number;
  oiNowUsd:      number;
}

/**
 * Ранний отчёт по накоплению OI — когда данных ещё < 14 дней.
 * Берём топ символов у которых OI вырос >= 15% при цене < 15%.
 * Минимум 3 снапшота чтобы отфильтровать однодневный шум.
 */
export async function getEarlyWhaleCandidates(): Promise<EarlyWhaleCandidate[]> {
  const rows = await db.execute<{
    symbol: string; days: string;
    oi_growth: string; price_chg: string; oi_now: string;
  }>(sql`
    WITH ranked AS (
      SELECT symbol, date, oi_usd, price_usd,
        ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY symbol ORDER BY date ASC)  AS rn_first,
        ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY symbol ORDER BY date DESC) AS rn_last,
        COUNT(*)     OVER (PARTITION BY symbol)                    AS total_days
      FROM oi_daily_snapshots
      WHERE oi_usd IS NOT NULL AND oi_usd > 0
    )
    SELECT
      f.symbol,
      f.total_days::int                                                         AS days,
      ROUND(((l.oi_usd - f.oi_usd) / NULLIF(f.oi_usd, 0) * 100)::numeric, 1)  AS oi_growth,
      ROUND(((l.price_usd - f.price_usd) / NULLIF(f.price_usd, 0) * 100)::numeric, 1) AS price_chg,
      ROUND((l.oi_usd / 1e6)::numeric, 1)                                       AS oi_now
    FROM ranked f
    JOIN ranked l ON f.symbol = l.symbol AND l.rn_last = 1
    WHERE f.rn_first = 1
      AND f.total_days >= 3
      AND f.total_days <= 13
      AND ((l.oi_usd - f.oi_usd) / NULLIF(f.oi_usd, 0) * 100) >= 15
      AND ((l.price_usd - f.price_usd) / NULLIF(f.price_usd, 0) * 100) > -15
      AND ((l.price_usd - f.price_usd) / NULLIF(f.price_usd, 0) * 100) < 15
      AND l.oi_usd >= 10e6
      AND l.oi_usd <= 10e9
    ORDER BY ((l.oi_usd - f.oi_usd) / NULLIF(f.oi_usd, 0)) DESC
    LIMIT 15
  `);

  return rows.rows.map(r => ({
    symbol:         r.symbol,
    days:           Number(r.days),
    oiGrowthPct:    Number(r.oi_growth),
    priceChangePct: Number(r.price_chg),
    oiNowUsd:       Number(r.oi_now),
  }));
}

// ── Daily alert dedup ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Хранит символы которым уже отправили whale-алерт сегодня (сбрасывается на следующий день).
const dailyAlertedSymbols = new Map<string, string>(); // symbol → YYYY-MM-DD

/**
 * Проверяет новых whale кандидатов и возвращает только тех, кому ещё не отправляли алерт
 * в течение последних 6 дней. Используется для ежедневных уведомлений.
 */
export async function getNewWhaleCandidatesForAlert(): Promise<WhaleAccumResult[]> {
  const candidates = await getAllWhaleAccumCandidates();
  const today = TODAY_UTC();
  const sixDaysAgo = new Date(Date.now() - 6 * 86400_000).toISOString().slice(0, 10);

  const fresh: WhaleAccumResult[] = [];
  for (const c of candidates) {
    const lastAlerted = dailyAlertedSymbols.get(c.symbol);
    // Пропускаем если уже алертили в течение 6 дней
    if (lastAlerted && lastAlerted >= sixDaysAgo) continue;
    dailyAlertedSymbols.set(c.symbol, today);
    fresh.push(c);
  }
  return fresh;
}

/**
 * Сколько символов уже имеет достаточно данных для whale-анализа?
 */
export async function getWhaleDataCoverage(): Promise<{ total: number; ready: number; oldestDate: string | null }> {
  // Drizzle не пробрасывает алиас window-функции в внешний запрос автоматически,
  // поэтому пишем весь запрос через sql`` чтобы избежать "column cnt does not exist".
  const rows = await db.execute<{ total: string; ready: string; oldest: string | null }>(sql`
    select
      count(distinct symbol)::int                                           as total,
      count(distinct symbol) filter (where cnt >= 14)::int                 as ready,
      min(date)                                                             as oldest
    from (
      select symbol, date, count(*) over (partition by symbol) as cnt
      from oi_daily_snapshots
    ) sub
  `);
  const stats = rows.rows;

  return {
    total:      Number(stats[0]?.total ?? 0),
    ready:      Number(stats[0]?.ready ?? 0),
    oldestDate: stats[0]?.oldest ?? null,
  };
}


================================================================
FILE: artifacts/api-server/src/lib/spike-watcher.ts
================================================================
import { logger } from "./logger.js";
import { getFutureTickers, getKlines, fetchCVD, type BingXTicker } from "./bingx.js";
import { broadcastRawAlert } from "./telegram.js";
import { trackSignal } from "./watcher.js";
import { db, signalsTable, trackedSignalsTable } from "@workspace/db";
import { inArray } from "drizzle-orm";

// ── Spike Watcher: real-time price spike detector ─────────────────────────────
// Polls ALL futures tickers every 60s (1 batch call), detects sudden price moves,
// validates with volume + CVD, fires impulse_buy / volume_breakout signals.
// Catches events like the SYN +7.69% in 30m that the 10-min cycle scanner misses.

const POLL_INTERVAL_MS     = 60_000;  // 60-second tick
const SPIKE_1M_THRESHOLD   = 3.0;    // % in 60s triggers candidate check
const SPIKE_3M_THRESHOLD   = 5.0;    // % in 3 min triggers candidate check
const VOL_MULT_MIN_IMPULSE  = 1.5;   // volMult for impulse_buy
const VOL_MULT_MIN_BREAKOUT = 2.0;   // volMult for volume_breakout
const BUYER_PCT_MIN         = 55.0;  // buyPct % for impulse_buy
const COOLDOWN_MS           = 20 * 60_000; // 20 min per-symbol cooldown
const MAX_PRICE24H_PCT      = 40;    // skip exhausted pumps >40% or <-40%
const MAX_CONCURRENT_VALID  = 5;     // max parallel validation requests

interface PriceEntry { price: number; ts: number }
interface SpikeEntry  { prices: PriceEntry[]; lastAlertAt: number }

const spikeMap = new Map<string, SpikeEntry>();
let running = false;

// ── State for /api/scanner/status ────────────────────────────────────────────
let lastTickAt: number | null = null;
let totalSpikesDetected = 0;

export interface SpikeWatcherStatus {
  running: boolean;
  lastTickAt: number | null;
  trackedSymbols: number;
  totalSpikesDetected: number;
}

export function getSpikeWatcherStatus(): SpikeWatcherStatus {
  return { running, lastTickAt, trackedSymbols: spikeMap.size, totalSpikesDetected };
}

// ── Helpers ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

function isCryptoFuture(sym: string): boolean {
  const base = sym.replace("-USDT", "");
  return !base.includes("2USD") && !base.startsWith("NCS") && !base.startsWith("NCC") && !base.startsWith("NCFX");
}

async function getActiveSymbols(): Promise<Set<string>> {
  try {
    const rows = await db
      .select({ symbol: trackedSignalsTable.symbol })
      .from(trackedSignalsTable)
      .where(inArray(trackedSignalsTable.status, ["active", "in_run", "accumulation", "re_entry"]));
    return new Set(rows.map(r => r.symbol));
  } catch {
    return new Set();
  }
}

async function calcVolMult(sym: string): Promise<{ volMult: number; curVol: number; avgVol: number } | null> {
  try {
    const klines = await getKlines(sym, "5m", 20);
    if (klines.length < 5) return null;
    const closed = klines.slice(0, -1);
    const cur = parseFloat(closed[closed.length - 1]?.volume ?? "0");
    const prev = closed.slice(-16, -1);
    if (prev.length === 0 || cur <= 0) return null;
    const avg = prev.reduce((s, k) => s + parseFloat(k.volume), 0) / prev.length;
    if (avg <= 0) return null;
    return { volMult: cur / avg, curVol: cur, avgVol: avg };
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

// ── Core: validate a spike candidate and fire signal if conditions met ────────

async function validateAndFire(
  sym: string,
  ticker: BingXTicker,
  spikePct: number,
): Promise<void> {
  const price    = parseFloat(ticker.lastPrice);
  const price24h = parseFloat(ticker.priceChangePercent);

  if (!price || price <= 0) return;

  const active = await getActiveSymbols();
  if (active.has(sym)) {
    logger.info({ sym }, "SpikeWatcher: уже активен — пропуск");
    return;
  }

  const [volData, cvd] = await Promise.all([
    calcVolMult(sym),
    fetchCVD(sym, 500),
  ]);

  if (!volData) {
    logger.info({ sym }, "SpikeWatcher: нет данных объёма — пропуск");
    return;
  }

  const { volMult, curVol, avgVol } = volData;
  const buyPct = cvd?.buyPct ?? 50;

  type PatternMatch = {
    sentimentKey: "impulse_buy" | "volume_breakout";
    sentiment:    string;
    traderAction: string;
    alphaScore:   number;
  };

  const match: PatternMatch | null =
    (volMult >= VOL_MULT_MIN_IMPULSE && buyPct >= BUYER_PCT_MIN)
      ? {
          sentimentKey: "impulse_buy",
          sentiment:    `Ценовой спайк +${spikePct.toFixed(1)}% за 60с`,
          traderAction: "Мгновенная покупка",
          alphaScore:   volMult >= 3 ? 9 : volMult >= 2 ? 8 : 7,
        }
      : (volMult >= VOL_MULT_MIN_BREAKOUT)
      ? {
          sentimentKey: "volume_breakout",
          sentiment:    `Спайк +${spikePct.toFixed(1)}% объём ×${volMult.toFixed(1)}`,
          traderAction: "Объёмный пробой",
          alphaScore:   6,
        }
      : null;

  if (!match) {
    logger.info(
      { sym, volMult: volMult.toFixed(2), buyPct: buyPct.toFixed(0), spikePct: spikePct.toFixed(1) },
      "SpikeWatcher: условия не выполнены (vol/CVD слабые) — пропуск",
    );
    return;
  }

  const { sentimentKey, sentiment, traderAction: _traderAction, alphaScore } = match;

  logger.info(
    { sym, spikePct: spikePct.toFixed(1), volMult: volMult.toFixed(2), buyPct: buyPct.toFixed(0), sentimentKey },
    "SpikeWatcher: ⚡ СПАЙК ПОДТВЕРЖДЁН",
  );

  totalSpikesDetected++;

  const tradingViewUrl = `https://www.tradingview.com/chart/?symbol=BINGX:${sym.replace("-", "")}`;
  const bingxUrl       = `https://bingx.com/en/trade/${sym.replace("-", "")}/`;

  let insertedId: number | undefined;
  for (let attempt = 1; attempt <= 3; attempt++) {
    try {
      const [ins] = await db.insert(signalsTable).values({
        symbol:             sym,
        type:               "alpha",
        value:              volMult,
        threshold:          VOL_MULT_MIN_IMPULSE,
        price,
        priceChangePercent: spikePct,
        volume24h:          curVol,
        avgVolume:          avgVol,
        openInterest:       null,
        oiChangePercent:    0,
        fundingRate:        null,
        tradingViewUrl,
        sentiment,
        sentimentKey,
        alphaScore,
        isContinuing:       false,
        cumulativeOiChange: 0,
      }).returning({ id: signalsTable.id });
      insertedId = ins?.id;
      break;
    } catch (err) {
      if (attempt >= 3) {
        logger.error({ err, sym }, "SpikeWatcher: insert failed after 3 attempts");
      } else {
        await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 2_000));
      }
    }
  }
  if (!insertedId) return;

  const tracked = await trackSignal({
    symbol:       sym,
    signalId:     insertedId,
    signalType:   "alpha",
    sentiment,
    sentimentKey,
    alphaScore,
    entryPrice:   price,
    entryOi:      null,
    entryCvd5m:   cvd?.positive ?? null,
    entryCvd1h:   null,
    entryCvd4h:   null,
  }).catch(err => {
    logger.warn({ err, sym }, "SpikeWatcher: trackSignal ошибка");
    return false as const;
  });

  if (tracked === false || tracked === "skip") {
    logger.info({ sym, tracked }, "SpikeWatcher: trackSignal skip — broadcast пропущен");
    return;
  }

  const emoji   = sentimentKey === "impulse_buy" ? "🚀" : "📊";
  const base    = sym.replace("-USDT", "");
  const volLine = `📊 Объём: ×${volMult.toFixed(1)} от среднего`;
  const cvdLine = cvd
    ? `🔵 Тейкеры: ${buyPct.toFixed(0)}% buy / ${cvd.sellPct.toFixed(0)}% sell`
    : `🔵 CVD: нет данных`;
  const p24hLine = `📈 Цена 24ч: ${price24h >= 0 ? "+" : ""}${price24h.toFixed(1)}%`;
  const patLine  = sentimentKey === "impulse_buy"
    ? `🚀 Паттерн: <b>impulse_buy</b>`
    : `📊 Паттерн: <b>volume_breakout</b>`;

  const msg = [
    `${emoji} <b>⚡ SPIKE — ${base}-USDT</b>`,
    ``,
    `🔺 Спайк: <b>+${spikePct.toFixed(1)}%</b> за ~1 мин`,
    `💰 Цена: <b>$${price}</b>`,
    volLine,
    cvdLine,
    p24hLine,
    patLine,
    ``,
    `🔗 <a href="${tradingViewUrl}">TradingView</a> | <a href="${bingxUrl}">BingX</a>`,
  ].join("\n");

  await broadcastRawAlert(msg).catch(err => {
    logger.warn({ err, sym }, "SpikeWatcher: broadcast failed");
  });

  logger.info({ sym, spikePct: spikePct.toFixed(1), sentimentKey, tracked }, "SpikeWatcher: ✅ сигнал отправлен");
}

// ── Main polling cycle ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

async function runSpikeCycle(): Promise<void> {
  const now = Date.now();
  lastTickAt = now;

  let tickers: BingXTicker[];
  try {
    tickers = await getFutureTickers();
  } catch (err) {
    logger.warn({ err }, "SpikeWatcher: не удалось получить тикеры");
    return;
  }

  const candidates: Array<{ sym: string; ticker: BingXTicker; spikePct: number }> = [];

  for (const ticker of tickers) {
    const sym = ticker.symbol;
    if (!sym.endsWith("-USDT") || !isCryptoFuture(sym)) continue;

    const price24hAbs = Math.abs(parseFloat(ticker.priceChangePercent) || 0);
    if (price24hAbs > MAX_PRICE24H_PCT) continue;

    const price = parseFloat(ticker.lastPrice);
    if (!price || price <= 0) continue;

    let entry = spikeMap.get(sym);
    if (!entry) {
      entry = { prices: [], lastAlertAt: 0 };
      spikeMap.set(sym, entry);
    }

    entry.prices.push({ price, ts: now });
    if (entry.prices.length > 4) entry.prices.shift();

    if (entry.prices.length < 2) continue;
    if (now - entry.lastAlertAt < COOLDOWN_MS) continue;

    const prev1m   = entry.prices[entry.prices.length - 2];
    const spike1m  = ((price - prev1m.price) / prev1m.price) * 100;

    const oldest   = entry.prices[0];
    const spike3m  = entry.prices.length >= 3
      ? ((price - oldest.price) / oldest.price) * 100
      : null;

    let spikePct: number | null = null;
    if (spike1m  >= SPIKE_1M_THRESHOLD) spikePct = spike1m;
    else if (spike3m !== null && spike3m >= SPIKE_3M_THRESHOLD) spikePct = spike3m;

    if (spikePct === null || spikePct <= 0) continue;

    entry.lastAlertAt = now;
    candidates.push({ sym, ticker, spikePct });

    logger.info(
      { sym, spike1m: spike1m.toFixed(2), spike3m: spike3m?.toFixed(2) },
      `SpikeWatcher: ⚡ кандидат +${spikePct.toFixed(1)}%`,
    );
  }

  if (candidates.length === 0) return;

  for (let i = 0; i < candidates.length; i += MAX_CONCURRENT_VALID) {
    const batch = candidates.slice(i, i + MAX_CONCURRENT_VALID);
    await Promise.allSettled(batch.map(c => validateAndFire(c.sym, c.ticker, c.spikePct)));
  }
}

// ── Lifecycle ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export function startSpikeWatcher(): void {
  if (running) return;
  running = true;
  logger.info("SpikeWatcher: старт (каждые 60с, порог 1m≥3% / 3m≥5%)");

  const loop = async () => {
    if (!running) return;
    try {
      await runSpikeCycle();
    } catch (err) {
      logger.error({ err }, "SpikeWatcher: необработанная ошибка цикла");
    }
    if (running) setTimeout(loop, POLL_INTERVAL_MS);
  };

  setTimeout(loop, 5_000);
}

export function stopSpikeWatcher(): void {
  running = false;
  logger.info("SpikeWatcher: остановлен");
}

FILE: artifacts/api-server/src/lib/spotPool.ts
================================================================
/**
 * Spot Liquidity Pool Cache
 *
 * Фоновый кэш спот-объёма и MCap для всего пула сканируемых токенов.
 * Обновляется каждые 30 минут пакетами — не блокирует основной скан.
 *
 * Используется как первичный фильтр в scanner.ts:
 *   if (!isSpotLiquid(symbol, 0.0005)) continue; // скип перед klines
 *
 * Если символ ещё не в кэше → пропускаем оптимистично (individual gate поймает).
 */

import { getCoinglassSpotVolume24h } from "./coinglass_spot_cvd.js";
import { fetchMarketCap } from "./whale_watch_v2.js";
import { logger } from "./logger.js";

const REFRESH_INTERVAL_MS = 30 * 60 * 1000; // 30 мин
const BATCH_SIZE          = 40;              // параллельных запросов за раз
const BATCH_DELAY_MS      = 600;             // пауза между пакетами (мс)

interface SpotEntry {
  spotVol24h: number | null;
  mcapUsd:    number | null;
  ts:         number;
}

const pool   = new Map<string, SpotEntry>();
let lastRefreshAt = 0;
let refreshRunning = false;

/**
 * Проверяет: является ли символ достаточно ликвидным по споту.
 * minPct — минимальный % MCap (0.0005 = 0.05%, 0.003 = 0.3%).
 *
 * Если символ НЕ в кэше → возвращает true (оптимистично — individual gate поймает).
 * Если spotVol24h null → возвращает false (нет данных о споте вообще).
 * Если mcapUsd null (CoinGecko недоступен) → возвращает true (оптимистично, как не в кэше).
 *   Малоликвидные токены стреляют сильнее — не режем их по абс. порогу без MCap.
 *   Остальные гейты (OI, CVD, Taker) поймают мусор.
 */
export function isSpotLiquid(symbol: string, minPct: number): boolean {
  const entry = pool.get(symbol);
  if (!entry) return true;
  if (entry.spotVol24h === null) return false;
  if (entry.mcapUsd === null) return true;
  return entry.spotVol24h >= entry.mcapUsd * minPct;
}

/**
 * Возвращает закэшированные данные по символу (или null).
 */
export function getSpotPoolEntry(symbol: string): SpotEntry | null {
  return pool.get(symbol) ?? null;
}

/**
 * Запускает фоновое обновление кэша для переданного списка символов.
 * Вызов не блокирует — сразу возвращает управление.
 * Пропускает если: обновление уже идёт ИЛИ прошло < 30 мин с последнего.
 */
export function triggerRefresh(symbols: string[]): void {
  const now = Date.now();
  if (refreshRunning) return;
  if (now - lastRefreshAt < REFRESH_INTERVAL_MS) return;

  void runRefresh(symbols);
}

async function runRefresh(symbols: string[]): Promise<void> {
  refreshRunning = true;
  lastRefreshAt  = Date.now();

  logger.info({ count: symbols.length }, "SpotPool: запуск обновления кэша спот-ликвидности");

  let updated = 0;
  let blocked = 0;

  for (let i = 0; i < symbols.length; i += BATCH_SIZE) {
    const batch = symbols.slice(i, i + BATCH_SIZE);

    await Promise.all(batch.map(async (symbol) => {
      try {
        const existing = pool.get(symbol);
        const [freshSpotVol, mcapRaw] = await Promise.all([
          getCoinglassSpotVolume24h(symbol).catch(() => null),
          fetchMarketCap(symbol).catch(() => null),
        ]);
        // Если свежие данные недоступны — сохраняем последнее известное значение.
        // SpotVol меняется плавно (rolling 24h), стale за 30 мин даёт правильный порог.
        // MCap меняется медленно — стale за часы корректен.
        const spotVol24h = freshSpotVol ?? existing?.spotVol24h ?? null;
        const mcapUsd    = mcapRaw?.mcap ?? existing?.mcapUsd    ?? null;
        // Не пишем в кэш если оба значения null и записи ещё нет.
        // Тогда isSpotLiquid вернёт true (оптимистично) вместо false.
        // Записываем только если есть хоть какие-то данные.
        if (spotVol24h !== null || mcapUsd !== null || existing) {
          pool.set(symbol, { spotVol24h, mcapUsd, ts: Date.now() });
        }

        if (spotVol24h !== null && mcapUsd !== null && spotVol24h < mcapUsd * 0.0015) {
          blocked++;
        }
        updated++;
      } catch {
        // non-critical — оставляем устаревший кэш если есть
      }
    }));

    if (i + BATCH_SIZE < symbols.length) {
      await new Promise<void>(r => setTimeout(r, BATCH_DELAY_MS));
    }
  }

  refreshRunning = false;
  logger.info({ updated, blocked, total: symbols.length }, "SpotPool: кэш обновлён");
}


================================================================
FILE: artifacts/api-server/src/lib/positionsReport.ts
================================================================
import { db, trackedSignalsTable } from "@workspace/db";
import type { TrackedSignal } from "@workspace/db";
import { inArray } from "drizzle-orm";
import { getFutureTickers } from "./bingx.js";
import { logger } from "./logger.js";

// ── Format helpers ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export function fmtP(p: number): string {
  if (p >= 1) return `$${p.toFixed(3)}`;
  if (p >= 0.01) return `$${p.toFixed(4)}`;
  if (p >= 0.001) return `$${p.toFixed(5)}`;
  return `$${p.toFixed(6)}`;
}

export function fmtPct(n: number): string {
  return `${n >= 0 ? "+" : ""}${n.toFixed(1)}%`;
}

function ageLabel(detectedAt: Date): string {
  const h = (Date.now() - detectedAt.getTime()) / 3_600_000;
  if (h < 24) return `${h.toFixed(0)}ч`;
  return `${(h / 24).toFixed(1)}д`;
}

function symBase(symbol: string): string {
  return symbol.replace("-USDT", "");
}

function symTag(symbol: string): string {
  return `#${symbol.replace("-USDT", "").replace(/[^A-Za-z0-9]/g, "")}`;
}

const SENTIMENT_LABELS: Record<string, string> = {
  silent_whale:           "Тихое накопление китов",
  trend_accumulation:     "Трендовое накопление",
  dip_accumulation:       "Накопление на дипе",
  spot_divergence:        "Спот-дивергенция",
  bullish_accumulation:   "Бычье накопление",
  accumulation_breakout:  "Пробой накопления",
  impulse_buy:            "Импульсная покупка",
  whale_futures:          "Кит + Розница",
  whale_watch:            "Китовый дозор",
  whale_watch_spot:       "Спот накопление китов",
  volume_breakout:        "Объёмный пробой",
  bingx_exclusive:        "BingX Exclusive",
  "4h_momentum":          "4H Моментум",
  quiet_oi_accumulation:  "Тихое OI накопление",
  volume_taker:           "Объёмный импульс",
  spot_absorption:        "Спот-поглощение",
  // legacy
  hidden_accumulation:    "Скрытое накопление",
};

function patTag(s: TrackedSignal): string {
  if (!s.sentimentKey) return "";
  const label = SENTIMENT_LABELS[s.sentimentKey] ?? s.sentimentKey;
  return ` [${label}]`;
}

function pnlOf(s: TrackedSignal, curPrice: number | undefined): number | null {
  if (!curPrice || !s.entryPrice || s.entryPrice === 0) return null;
  const diff = curPrice - s.entryPrice;
  return (s.isShort ? -diff : diff) / s.entryPrice * 100;
}

function peakPnlOf(s: TrackedSignal): number | null {
  if (!s.peakPrice || !s.entryPrice || s.entryPrice === 0) return null;
  return ((s.peakPrice - s.entryPrice) / s.entryPrice) * 100;
}

// ── Единственный форматтер открытых позиций ───────────────────────────────────
// Чистая функция — принимает готовые данные, не делает запросов к DB/API.
// Используется в: buildPositionsStatusText, sendDailyReport, /status кнопка.
export function formatOpenPositionLines(
  allOpen: TrackedSignal[],
  priceMap: Map<string, number>,
): string[] {
  const lines: string[] = [];

  const inRun    = allOpen.filter(s => s.status === "in_run");
  const watching = allOpen.filter(s =>
    s.status === "active" || s.status === "accumulation" || s.status === "re_entry"
  );

  if (inRun.length > 0) {
    lines.push(`🚀 <b>В полёте (Moonshot) — ${inRun.length}:</b>`);
    for (const s of inRun) {
      const curPrice = priceMap.get(s.symbol);
      const pnl      = pnlOf(s, curPrice);
      const peak     = peakPnlOf(s);
      const priceStr = curPrice && s.entryPrice
        ? ` | ${fmtP(s.entryPrice)} → ${fmtP(curPrice)} (${fmtPct(pnl ?? 0)})`
        : "";
      const peakStr  = peak != null ? ` | Пик: +${peak.toFixed(1)}%` : "";
      const stopStr  = s.trailingStopLevel
        ? ` | Trailing: ${fmtP(s.trailingStopLevel)}`
        : "";
      lines.push(
        `  • <b>${symBase(s.symbol)}</b>${patTag(s)} ${symTag(s.symbol)}${priceStr}${peakStr}${stopStr} | ${ageLabel(s.detectedAt)}`
      );
    }
    lines.push(``);
  }

  if (watching.length > 0) {
    lines.push(`👁 <b>В наблюдении — ${watching.length}:</b>`);
    for (const s of watching) {
      const curPrice = priceMap.get(s.symbol);
      const pnl      = pnlOf(s, curPrice);
      const priceStr = curPrice && s.entryPrice
        ? ` | ${fmtP(s.entryPrice)} → ${fmtP(curPrice)} (${fmtPct(pnl ?? 0)})`
        : "";
      lines.push(
        `  • <b>${symBase(s.symbol)}</b>${patTag(s)} ${symTag(s.symbol)}${priceStr} | ${ageLabel(s.detectedAt)}`
      );
    }
    lines.push(``);
  }

  return lines;
}

// ── Полный статус-текст — рестарт-уведомление, 4ч авто-апдейт, кнопка /status ─
export async function buildPositionsStatusText(): Promise<string> {
  const dbHost = process.env.DATABASE_URL?.match(/@([^/]+)\//)?.[1] ?? "unknown";
  let allOpen = await db.select()
    .from(trackedSignalsTable)
    .where(inArray(trackedSignalsTable.status, ["active", "in_run", "accumulation", "re_entry"]));

  logger.info({
    count: allOpen.length,
    statuses: allOpen.map(s => ({ symbol: s.symbol, status: s.status })),
    dbHost,
  }, "buildPositionsStatusText: DEBUG query result");

  // Если получили 0 позиций — перепроверяем через 30с.
  // Причина: под нагрузкой (первый скан 551 токена) pg-pool может вернуть пустой
  // result SET без exception вместо connection timeout. Retry отсеивает ложные нули.
  if (allOpen.length === 0) {
    await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 30_000));
    allOpen = await db.select()
      .from(trackedSignalsTable)
      .where(inArray(trackedSignalsTable.status, ["active", "in_run", "accumulation", "re_entry"]));
    logger.info({
      count: allOpen.length,
      statuses: allOpen.map(s => ({ symbol: s.symbol, status: s.status })),
      dbHost,
    }, "buildPositionsStatusText: DEBUG retry query result");
  }

  if (allOpen.length === 0) {
    return [
      `📊 <b>Открытых позиций нет</b>`,
      ``,
      `Сканер ищет сигналы в режиме реального времени.`,
      `Сигнал придёт когда условия выполнятся.`,
    ].join("\n");
  }

  const priceMap = new Map<string, number>();
  try {
    const tickers = await getFutureTickers() as { symbol: string; lastPrice: string }[];
    for (const t of tickers) priceMap.set(t.symbol, parseFloat(t.lastPrice) || 0);
  } catch { /* non-critical — покажем без цен */ }

  const moscowTime = new Date().toLocaleString("ru-RU", {
    timeZone: "Europe/Moscow",
    day: "2-digit", month: "long", hour: "2-digit", minute: "2-digit",
  });

  return [
    `📊 <b>Открытые позиции</b> — <i>${moscowTime} МСК</i>`,
    ``,
    ...formatOpenPositionLines(allOpen, priceMap),
    `📋 Всего открытых: <b>${allOpen.length}</b> | Авто-апдейт каждые 4ч`,
  ].join("\n");
}


================================================================
FILE: artifacts/api-server/src/lib/orderbook_external.ts
================================================================
// ── Cross-exchange market data aggregator ────────────────────────────────────
// Order book: BingX (primary) + Binance Futures + OKX
//   bid/ask wall cross-confirmed when BingX AND (Binance OR OKX) both see it.
// Volume:     Binance Futures + Bybit + OKX + Bitget (30m klines, last 6 candles)
//   cross-confirmed when ≥ 2 out of 4 external exchanges show volMult ≥ 1.5x.

import { logger } from "./logger.js";
import { getOrderBookAnalysis, type OrderBookAnalysis } from "./bingx.js";

// ── Symbol converters ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

function toBinanceSymbol(s: string): string {
  return s.replace("-", ""); // BTC-USDT → BTCUSDT
}

function toBybitSymbol(s: string): string {
  return s.replace("-", ""); // BTC-USDT → BTCUSDT (linear perpetual)
}

function toOKXSymbol(s: string): string {
  return s.replace("-USDT", "-USDT-SWAP"); // BTC-USDT → BTC-USDT-SWAP
}

function toBitgetSymbol(s: string): string {
  return s.replace("-", ""); // BTC-USDT → BTCUSDT
}

// ── Per-exchange result ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────

interface ExchangeOBResult {
  largestBidWallUsd: number;
  largestAskWallUsd: number;
  bidVolumeUsd:      number;
  askVolumeUsd:      number;
  bidAskRatio:       number;
}

// ── Public type ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export interface CrossExchangeOrderBook extends OrderBookAnalysis {
  /** Bid wall confirmed by BingX AND (Binance OR OKX) */
  bidWallCrossConfirmed: boolean;
  /** Ask wall confirmed by BingX AND (Binance OR OKX) */
  askWallCrossConfirmed: boolean;
  /** How many exchanges see a significant bid wall (max 3) */
  bidWallExchangeCount: number;
  /** Exchanges that responded */
  exchanges: string[];
}

// ── Fetch helpers ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

async function getBinanceOrderBook(symbol: string, limit: number): Promise<ExchangeOBResult | null> {
  try {
    const controller = new AbortController();
    const timer = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), 4000);
    const res = await fetch(
      `https://fapi.binance.com/fapi/v1/depth?symbol=${toBinanceSymbol(symbol)}&limit=${limit}`,
      { signal: controller.signal },
    );
    clearTimeout(timer);
    if (!res.ok) return null;

    const data = await res.json() as { bids?: [string, string][]; asks?: [string, string][] };
    const rawBids = data.bids ?? [];
    const rawAsks = data.asks ?? [];
    if (!rawBids.length && !rawAsks.length) return null;

    const bids = rawBids.map(([p, q]) => parseFloat(p) * parseFloat(q));
    const asks = rawAsks.map(([p, q]) => parseFloat(p) * parseFloat(q));

    const bidVolumeUsd      = bids.reduce((s, v) => s + v, 0);
    const askVolumeUsd      = asks.reduce((s, v) => s + v, 0);
    const bidAskRatio       = askVolumeUsd > 0 ? bidVolumeUsd / askVolumeUsd : 0;
    const largestBidWallUsd = bids.length ? Math.max(...bids) : 0;
    const largestAskWallUsd = asks.length ? Math.max(...asks) : 0;

    return { largestBidWallUsd, largestAskWallUsd, bidVolumeUsd, askVolumeUsd, bidAskRatio };
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

async function getOKXOrderBook(symbol: string, limit: number): Promise<ExchangeOBResult | null> {
  try {
    const controller = new AbortController();
    const timer = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), 4000);
    const res = await fetch(
      `https://www.okx.com/api/v5/market/books?instId=${toOKXSymbol(symbol)}&sz=${limit}`,
      { signal: controller.signal },
    );
    clearTimeout(timer);
    if (!res.ok) return null;

    const data = await res.json() as {
      data?: Array<{
        bids?: [string, string, string, string][];
        asks?: [string, string, string, string][];
      }>;
    };
    const entry = data.data?.[0];
    if (!entry) return null;

    const rawBids = entry.bids ?? [];
    const rawAsks = entry.asks ?? [];
    if (!rawBids.length && !rawAsks.length) return null;

    const bids = rawBids.map(([p, q]) => parseFloat(p) * parseFloat(q));
    const asks = rawAsks.map(([p, q]) => parseFloat(p) * parseFloat(q));

    const bidVolumeUsd      = bids.reduce((s, v) => s + v, 0);
    const askVolumeUsd      = asks.reduce((s, v) => s + v, 0);
    const bidAskRatio       = askVolumeUsd > 0 ? bidVolumeUsd / askVolumeUsd : 0;
    const largestBidWallUsd = bids.length ? Math.max(...bids) : 0;
    const largestAskWallUsd = asks.length ? Math.max(...asks) : 0;

    return { largestBidWallUsd, largestAskWallUsd, bidVolumeUsd, askVolumeUsd, bidAskRatio };
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

// ── Wall significance ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// If volumeUsd24h is provided: use bidWallUsd / volumeUsd24h (relative threshold).
// Fallback: use bidAskRatio when 24h volume is not available.

const WALL_VOL_RATIO = 0.005; // 0.5% of 24h volume → significant wall

function seenBidWall(r: ExchangeOBResult, volumeUsd24h?: number): boolean {
  if (volumeUsd24h && volumeUsd24h > 0) return r.largestBidWallUsd / volumeUsd24h >= WALL_VOL_RATIO;
  return r.bidAskRatio >= 1.8;
}

function seenAskWall(r: ExchangeOBResult, volumeUsd24h?: number): boolean {
  if (volumeUsd24h && volumeUsd24h > 0) return r.largestAskWallUsd / volumeUsd24h >= WALL_VOL_RATIO;
  return r.bidAskRatio <= 0.6;
}

// ── Main export ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

/**
 * Fetches order book depth from BingX, Binance Futures, and OKX simultaneously.
 * BingX is the primary source (returns null if BingX fails).
 * Cross-confirmation: bid/ask wall confirmed when BingX AND (Binance OR OKX) agree.
 *
 * @param symbol      BingX format: "BTC-USDT"
 * @param limit       Depth levels per exchange (default 20)
 * @param volumeUsd24h Optional 24h USDT volume for relative wall threshold
 */
export async function getCrossExchangeOrderBook(
  symbol: string,
  limit = 20,
  volumeUsd24h?: number,
): Promise<CrossExchangeOrderBook | null> {
  const [bingxRes, binanceRes, okxRes] = await Promise.allSettled([
    getOrderBookAnalysis(symbol, limit),
    getBinanceOrderBook(symbol, limit),
    getOKXOrderBook(symbol, limit),
  ]);

  const bingx   = bingxRes.status   === "fulfilled" ? bingxRes.value   : null;
  const binance = binanceRes.status === "fulfilled" ? binanceRes.value : null;
  const okx     = okxRes.status     === "fulfilled" ? okxRes.value     : null;

  if (!bingx) {
    logger.warn({ symbol }, "CrossOB: BingX unavailable — skipping");
    return null;
  }

  const exchanges: string[] = ["bingx"];
  if (binance) exchanges.push("binance");
  if (okx)     exchanges.push("okx");

  const binanceBid = binance ? seenBidWall(binance, volumeUsd24h) : false;
  const okxBid     = okx     ? seenBidWall(okx,     volumeUsd24h) : false;
  const binanceAsk = binance ? seenAskWall(binance, volumeUsd24h) : false;
  const okxAsk     = okx     ? seenAskWall(okx,     volumeUsd24h) : false;

  const bidWallCrossConfirmed = binanceBid || okxBid;
  const askWallCrossConfirmed = binanceAsk || okxAsk;
  const bidWallExchangeCount  = 1 + (binanceBid ? 1 : 0) + (okxBid ? 1 : 0);

  logger.debug({
    symbol,
    exchanges,
    bingxBias: bingx.bias,
    bidWallCrossConfirmed,
    askWallCrossConfirmed,
    bidWallExchangeCount,
    volRelative: volumeUsd24h ? `${(bingx.largestBidWallUsd / volumeUsd24h * 100).toFixed(3)}%` : "n/a",
  }, "CrossOB: aggregated");

  return {
    ...bingx,
    bidWallCrossConfirmed,
    askWallCrossConfirmed,
    bidWallExchangeCount,
    exchanges,
  };
}

// ── Cross-exchange volume confirmation ────────────────────────────────────────
// Fetches 30m klines from 4 external exchanges (Binance Futures, Bybit, OKX, Bitget).
// Each computes volMult = currentCandle / avg(last 6 closed candles).
// cross-confirmed if ≥ 2 exchanges report volMult ≥ 1.5x.
// finalVolMult = max(bingxVolMult, avg of confirming externals).

const KLINES_TIMEOUT_MS          = 3000;
const CROSS_VOL_SPIKE_THRESHOLD  = 1.5; // external exchange volMult threshold to count as "confirmed"
const CROSS_VOL_MIN_CONFIRMATIONS = 2;  // need ≥ 2 of 4 external exchanges

export interface ExchangeVolResult {
  exchange: string;
  volMult: number;
}

export interface CrossExchangeVolConfirmation {
  /** External exchanges that confirmed the spike (volMult ≥ CROSS_VOL_SPIKE_THRESHOLD) */
  confirming: ExchangeVolResult[];
  /** All external exchanges that responded */
  all: ExchangeVolResult[];
  /** true if ≥ 2 out of 4 external exchanges confirmed */
  crossConfirmed: boolean;
  /** max(bingxVolMult, avg of confirming externals) — bingxVolMult if not confirmed */
  finalVolMult: number;
}

async function getBinanceVolMult(symbol: string): Promise<number | null> {
  try {
    const controller = new AbortController();
    const t = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), KLINES_TIMEOUT_MS);
    const res = await fetch(
      `https://fapi.binance.com/fapi/v1/klines?symbol=${toBinanceSymbol(symbol)}&interval=30m&limit=7`,
      { signal: controller.signal },
    );
    clearTimeout(t);
    if (!res.ok) return null;
    const rows = await res.json() as unknown[][];
    if (rows.length < 7) return null;
    // Row: [openTime, o, h, l, c, baseVol, closeTime, quoteVol, ...]
    // quoteVol (index 7) is in USDT — newest is last
    const current = parseFloat(rows[rows.length - 1][7] as string);
    const hist    = rows.slice(0, 6).map(r => parseFloat(r[7] as string));
    const avg     = hist.reduce((s, v) => s + v, 0) / hist.length;
    return avg > 0 ? current / avg : null;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

async function getBybitVolMult(symbol: string): Promise<number | null> {
  try {
    const controller = new AbortController();
    const t = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), KLINES_TIMEOUT_MS);
    const res = await fetch(
      `https://api.bybit.com/v5/market/kline?category=linear&symbol=${toBybitSymbol(symbol)}&interval=30&limit=7`,
      { signal: controller.signal },
    );
    clearTimeout(t);
    if (!res.ok) return null;
    const data = await res.json() as { result?: { list?: string[][] } };
    const list = data.result?.list;
    if (!list || list.length < 7) return null;
    // Bybit returns newest-first. Row: [startTime, o, h, l, c, volume, turnover]
    // turnover (index 6) = USDT volume
    const current = parseFloat(list[0][6]);
    const hist    = list.slice(1, 7).map(r => parseFloat(r[6]));
    const avg     = hist.reduce((s, v) => s + v, 0) / hist.length;
    return avg > 0 ? current / avg : null;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

async function getOKXVolMult(symbol: string): Promise<number | null> {
  try {
    const controller = new AbortController();
    const t = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), KLINES_TIMEOUT_MS);
    const res = await fetch(
      `https://www.okx.com/api/v5/market/candles?instId=${toOKXSymbol(symbol)}&bar=30m&limit=7`,
      { signal: controller.signal },
    );
    clearTimeout(t);
    if (!res.ok) return null;
    const data = await res.json() as { data?: string[][] };
    const list = data.data;
    if (!list || list.length < 7) return null;
    // OKX returns newest-first. Row: [ts, o, h, l, c, vol, volCcy, volCcyQuote, confirm]
    // volCcyQuote (index 7) = USDT volume
    const current = parseFloat(list[0][7]);
    const hist    = list.slice(1, 7).map(r => parseFloat(r[7]));
    const avg     = hist.reduce((s, v) => s + v, 0) / hist.length;
    return avg > 0 ? current / avg : null;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

async function getBitgetVolMult(symbol: string): Promise<number | null> {
  try {
    const controller = new AbortController();
    const t = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), KLINES_TIMEOUT_MS);
    const res = await fetch(
      `https://api.bitget.com/api/v2/mix/market/candles?symbol=${toBitgetSymbol(symbol)}&productType=USDT-FUTURES&granularity=30m&limit=7`,
      { signal: controller.signal },
    );
    clearTimeout(t);
    if (!res.ok) return null;
    const data = await res.json() as { data?: string[][] };
    const list = data.data;
    if (!list || list.length < 7) return null;
    // Bitget returns newest-first. Row: [ts, o, h, l, c, baseVol, quoteVol]
    // quoteVol (index 6) = USDT volume
    const current = parseFloat(list[0][6]);
    const hist    = list.slice(1, 7).map(r => parseFloat(r[6]));
    const avg     = hist.reduce((s, v) => s + v, 0) / hist.length;
    return avg > 0 ? current / avg : null;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

/**
 * Fetches 30m klines from Binance Futures, Bybit, OKX, and Bitget simultaneously.
 * Only runs if BingX already shows elevated volume (guard: caller should check volMult ≥ 1.3).
 *
 * @param symbol       BingX format: "BTC-USDT"
 * @param bingxVolMult Already-computed BingX volume multiplier
 */
export async function getCrossExchangeVolMult(
  symbol: string,
  bingxVolMult: number,
): Promise<CrossExchangeVolConfirmation> {
  const [binanceRes, bybitRes, okxRes, bitgetRes] = await Promise.allSettled([
    getBinanceVolMult(symbol),
    getBybitVolMult(symbol),
    getOKXVolMult(symbol),
    getBitgetVolMult(symbol),
  ]);

  const raw = [
    { exchange: "binance", res: binanceRes },
    { exchange: "bybit",   res: bybitRes },
    { exchange: "okx",     res: okxRes },
    { exchange: "bitget",  res: bitgetRes },
  ] as const;

  const all: ExchangeVolResult[] = [];
  for (const { exchange, res } of raw) {
    if (res.status === "fulfilled" && res.value !== null) {
      all.push({ exchange, volMult: res.value });
    }
  }

  const confirming   = all.filter(r => r.volMult >= CROSS_VOL_SPIKE_THRESHOLD);
  const crossConfirmed = confirming.length >= CROSS_VOL_MIN_CONFIRMATIONS;

  let finalVolMult = bingxVolMult;
  if (crossConfirmed) {
    const avgExternal = confirming.reduce((s, r) => s + r.volMult, 0) / confirming.length;
    finalVolMult = Math.max(bingxVolMult, avgExternal);
  }

  logger.debug({
    symbol,
    bingxVolMult: bingxVolMult.toFixed(2),
    external: all.map(r => `${r.exchange}:${r.volMult.toFixed(2)}`).join(", ") || "none",
    confirming: confirming.length,
    crossConfirmed,
    finalVolMult: finalVolMult.toFixed(2),
  }, "CrossVol: aggregated");

  return { confirming, all, crossConfirmed, finalVolMult };
}

FILE: artifacts/api-server/src/lib/orderbookScanner.ts

import { db, settingsTable, subscribersTable } from "@workspace/db";
import { eq, sql } from "drizzle-orm";
import { logger } from "./logger.js";
import { getCrossExchangeOrderBook } from "./orderbook_external.js";
import { sendMessage } from "./telegram.js";

async function getOrderBookTargets(): Promise<(number | string)[]> {
  const targets: (number | string)[] = [];
  try {
    const [settings] = await db.select().from(settingsTable).limit(1);
    if (settings?.channelId) targets.push(settings.channelId);
    const subs = await db.select({ chatId: subscribersTable.chatId })
      .from(subscribersTable)
      .where(eq(subscribersTable.active, true));
    for (const s of subs) targets.push(s.chatId);
  } catch { /* ignore */ }
  return targets;
}

async function scanWatchlistOrderbooks(): Promise<void> {
  logger.info("OrderBook scanner cycle start");
  try {
    const [settings] = await db.select().from(settingsTable).limit(1);
    const watchlist = settings?.watchlist ?? "";
    if (!watchlist.trim()) {
      logger.info("OrderBook scanner: watchlist empty, skipping");
      return;
    }

    const symbols = watchlist.split(",").map(s => s.trim().toUpperCase()).filter(Boolean);
    const targets = await getOrderBookTargets();

    for (const symbol of symbols) {
      try {
        const ob = await getCrossExchangeOrderBook(symbol, 50);
        if (!ob) continue;

        if (ob.bias === "strong_bull" && ob.bidWallCrossConfirmed) {
          const exchLabel = ob.exchanges.join("+").toUpperCase();
          const msg = [
            `🐋 <b>Крупная стена в стакане!</b>`,
            `Символ: <b>${symbol}</b>`,
            `Стена: <b>$${Math.round(ob.largestBidWallUsd).toLocaleString("ru")}</b> @ ${ob.largestBidWallPrice}`,
            `Перекос: ${ob.bidAskRatio.toFixed(2)} (Bid/Ask)`,
            `Подтверждено: <b>${exchLabel}</b> (${ob.bidWallExchangeCount} биржи)`,
            `#${symbol.replace("-USDT", "").toLowerCase()} #orderbook_wall`,
          ].join("\n");
          for (const t of targets) {
            await sendMessage(t, msg).catch(() => {});
          }
          logger.info({ symbol, wallUsd: ob.largestBidWallUsd, exchanges: ob.exchanges }, "OrderBook: cross-confirmed bid wall detected");
        }
      } catch (err) {
        logger.error({ symbol, err }, "OrderBook scanner: error for symbol");
      }
      await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 1000));
    }
  } catch (err) {
    logger.error({ err }, "OrderBook scanner cycle error");
  }
}

export async function startOrderBookScanner(): Promise<void> {
  logger.info("Starting Order Book Scanner (watchlist, 5m)");
  setInterval(() => {
    scanWatchlistOrderbooks().catch(err =>
      logger.error({ err }, "OrderBook scanner interval error")
    );
  }, 5 * 60_000);
  await scanWatchlistOrderbooks();
}

import { logger } from "./logger.js";

export interface LiquidationResult {
  liquidationsUsd: number | null;
  avgLiquidationsUsd: number | null;
  multiple: number | null;
  available: boolean;
}

// In-memory store of recent liquidation values per symbol for computing average
const liqHistory = new Map<string, number[]>();
const MAX_HISTORY = 8; // keep last 8 readings (4h at 30m intervals)

// Normalize symbol for Coinglass: BTC-USDT → BTCUSDT
function toCglSymbol(symbol: string): string {
  return symbol.replace("-", "");
}

export async function getLiquidations(symbol: string): Promise<LiquidationResult> {
  const apiKey = process.env.COINGLASS_API_KEY;
  if (!apiKey) {
    return { liquidationsUsd: null, avgLiquidationsUsd: null, multiple: null, available: false };
  }

  const cglSymbol = toCglSymbol(symbol);

  try {
    // Coinglass liquidation chart for last 30m
    const res = await fetch(
      `https://open-api.coinglass.com/public/v2/liquidation_history?ex=Binance&pair=${cglSymbol}&interval=0.5h&limit=2`,
      {
        headers: {
          "coinglassSecret": apiKey,
          Accept: "application/json",
        },
        signal: AbortSignal.timeout(5000),
      }
    );

    if (!res.ok) {
      return { liquidationsUsd: null, avgLiquidationsUsd: null, multiple: null, available: false };
    }

    const data = await res.json() as {
      code: string;
      data: {
        shortLiquidationUsd: number;
        longLiquidationUsd: number;
        t: number;
      }[];
    };

    if (data.code !== "0" || !data.data?.length) {
      return { liquidationsUsd: null, avgLiquidationsUsd: null, multiple: null, available: false };
    }

    // Get the most recent interval's short liquidations
    const latest = data.data[data.data.length - 1];
    const currentLiqUsd = (latest.shortLiquidationUsd ?? 0) + (latest.longLiquidationUsd ?? 0);

    // Track history to compute rolling average
    const history = liqHistory.get(symbol) ?? [];
    if (history.length >= MAX_HISTORY) history.shift();
    history.push(currentLiqUsd);
    liqHistory.set(symbol, history);

    const avgLiq = history.length > 1
      ? history.slice(0, -1).reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / (history.length - 1)
      : currentLiqUsd;

    const multiple = avgLiq > 0 ? currentLiqUsd / avgLiq : null;

    return {
      liquidationsUsd: currentLiqUsd,
      avgLiquidationsUsd: avgLiq,
      multiple,
      available: true,
    };
  } catch (err) {
    logger.warn({ symbol, err }, "Coinglass liquidations fetch failed");
    return { liquidationsUsd: null, avgLiquidationsUsd: null, multiple: null, available: false };
  }
}

FILE: artifacts/api-server/src/lib/trending.ts
================================================================
import { logger } from "./logger.js";
import { reportApiSuccess, reportApiFailure } from "./apiHealth.js";

export interface TrendingResult {
  rank: number | null;
  source: "CMC" | "CoinGecko" | null;
  available: boolean;
}

// Normalize symbol for comparison: "BTC-USDT" → "BTC", "BTCUSDT" → "BTC"
function normalizeSymbol(symbol: string): string {
  return symbol.replace(/-USDT$/, "").replace(/USDT$/, "").toUpperCase();
}

// CoinGecko trending — free, no API key needed, returns top 7 trending
let cgCacheTime = 0;
let cgCache: { id: string; symbol: string; rank: number }[] = [];
const CG_CACHE_TTL = 15 * 60 * 1000; // 15 min

async function getCoinGeckoTrending(): Promise<{ id: string; symbol: string; rank: number }[]> {
  if (Date.now() - cgCacheTime < CG_CACHE_TTL && cgCache.length > 0) return cgCache;

  try {
    const res = await fetch("https://api.coingecko.com/api/v3/search/trending", {
      headers: { Accept: "application/json" },
      signal: AbortSignal.timeout(5000),
    });
    if (!res.ok) {
      reportApiFailure("coingecko", res.status);
      return cgCache;
    }

    const data = await res.json() as {
      coins: { item: { id: string; symbol: string; market_cap_rank: number } }[];
    };

    cgCache = data.coins.map((c, i) => ({
      id: c.item.id,
      symbol: c.item.symbol.toUpperCase(),
      rank: i + 1,
    }));
    cgCacheTime = Date.now();
    reportApiSuccess("coingecko");
    return cgCache;
  } catch {
    reportApiFailure("coingecko");
    return cgCache;
  }
}

// CMC Trending — requires CMC_API_KEY (free Basic plan)
let cmcCacheTime = 0;
let cmcCache: { symbol: string; rank: number }[] = [];

async function getCMCTrending(): Promise<{ symbol: string; rank: number }[]> {
  const apiKey = process.env.CMC_API_KEY;
  if (!apiKey) return [];

  if (Date.now() - cmcCacheTime < CG_CACHE_TTL && cmcCache.length > 0) return cmcCache;

  try {
    const res = await fetch(
      "https://pro-api.coinmarketcap.com/v1/cryptocurrency/trending/latest?limit=50",
      {
        headers: { "X-CMC_PRO_API_KEY": apiKey, Accept: "application/json" },
        signal: AbortSignal.timeout(5000),
      }
    );
    if (!res.ok) return cmcCache;

    const data = await res.json() as {
      data: { symbol: string; cmc_rank: number }[];
    };

    cmcCache = data.data.map((c, i) => ({
      symbol: c.symbol.toUpperCase(),
      rank: i + 1,
    }));
    cmcCacheTime = Date.now();
    return cmcCache;
  } catch {
    return cmcCache;
  }
}

export async function getTrendingRank(symbol: string): Promise<TrendingResult> {
  const base = normalizeSymbol(symbol);

  // Try CMC first if key available
  const cmcData = await getCMCTrending();
  if (cmcData.length > 0) {
    const match = cmcData.find(c => c.symbol === base);
    return {
      rank: match?.rank ?? null,
      source: "CMC",
      available: true,
    };
  }

  // Fallback to CoinGecko (free, top 7)
  const cgData = await getCoinGeckoTrending();
  if (cgData.length > 0) {
    const match = cgData.find(c => c.symbol === base);
    return {
      rank: match?.rank ?? null,
      source: "CoinGecko",
      available: true,
    };
  }

  return { rank: null, source: null, available: false };
}

FILE: lib/db/src/index.ts
================================================================
import { drizzle } from "drizzle-orm/node-postgres";
import pg from "pg";
import * as schema from "./schema";

const { Pool } = pg;

if (!process.env.DATABASE_URL) {
  throw new Error(
    "DATABASE_URL must be set. Did you forget to provision a database?",
  );
}

export const pool = new Pool({
  connectionString: process.env.DATABASE_URL,
  // Neon serverless: держим малый пул — большое число одновременных соединений
  // вызывает "Authentication timed out" при cold-start и исчерпании слотов.
  max: 3,
  // Neon закрывает TCP на своей стороне через ~5 мин; keepAlive на Node-стороне
  // не помогает (Neon NAT/proxy режет соединение раньше) — отключаем.
  keepAlive: false,
  // Отдаём idle-соединение обратно в пул быстро — чтобы Neon не успел его убить,
  // а пул не пытался переиспользовать мёртвое соединение.
  idleTimeoutMillis: 10_000,
  // Лимит на ожидание свободного слота в пуле.
  connectionTimeoutMillis: 15_000,
  allowExitOnIdle: false,
});

// Ловим ошибки на idle-клиентах (например, Neon закрыл соединение пока оно лежало в pool).
// Без этого Node.js кидает unhandled 'error' event и процесс может упасть.
pool.on("error", (err) => {
  void err;
});

export const db = drizzle(pool, { schema });

export * from "./schema";

        lastNetBuyPct:
          type: number
          nullable: true

          type: number
          nullable: true
        lastOi30m:
          type: number
          nullable: true
        lastOi1h:
          type: number
          nullable: true
        lastOi4h:
          type: number
          nullable: true
        lastOi24h:
          type: number
          nullable: true
        priceMovePct:
          type: number
          description: Price drift since first seen in prescan (%)
          nullable: true
        lastPrice1h:
          type: number
          description: Price change over the last 1h (2x30m candles), from BingX klines (%)
          nullable: true
        lastCvd15m:
          type: boolean
          nullable: true
          description: Spot CVD 15m verdict (3 of 4 periods positive)
        lastCvd1h:
          type: boolean
          nullable: true
          description: Spot CVD 1h verdict
        lastCvd4h:
          type: boolean
          nullable: true
          description: Spot CVD 4h verdict
        cgTimedOut:
          type: boolean
          nullable: true
          description: true = last CoinGlass request timed out (not a true BingX-only token)
        firstPrice:
          type: number
          nullable: true
          description: Price when first seen in prescan (entry price)
        lastOiAt:
          type: number
          nullable: true
          description: Unix ms timestamp of last OI data update for this token (null = never scanned with live OI)
      required:
        - symbol
        - cycleCount
        - bestRank
        - lastPrice24h
        - lastPrice
        - lastScore
        - firstSeenAt
